# ZOOM multi-effects

Canonical root-level brand document. New research is added here, not to dated findings or progress-part Markdown files. **Checked 2026-10-15.** Existing ZOOM records in the legacy comparison pages still require a complete, lossless merge; the G11 entry below is new and does not certify that the brand migration is finished. Never create GitHub Actions workflows for this research.

## G11

Guitar and bass multi-effects; large; built-in expression. Specifications below use ZOOM's own V2 operation/effect documents and current support page. A first-pass record with unresolved fields is not fully verified.

<!-- comparison evidence begins -->
| Required field | Verified specification / remaining uncertainty |
|---|---|
| Built-in / external expression | Built-in pedal with toe-operated effect on/off; one CONTROL IN for an external ZOOM FP02M. Separate rear power switch is not an expression control. [G11-M] |
| Physical controls | **11 footswitches**: five effect switches plus six SCROLL/PATCH/BANK navigation switches; **1 separate AMPLIFIER button**. Six amp rotary controls and a rear MASTER are explicit; complete rotary total across the five effect panels remains **unverified** pending successful visual inspection. Toe switch and rear power switch are separate. Marketing's five stomp switches must not erase the navigation switches. [G11-M, pp.11–14,23] |
| Main display | **5-inch, 800×480 TFT color touchscreen**. [G11-P, G11-M p.104] |
| Additional displays | **Six 128×32 dot-matrix LCDs**, one amp plus five effect displays. Their physical diagonals, explicit color mode and touch status are not established by the numerical specification; do not assign the main screen's five inches to them. [G11-M] |
| Dimensions / weight | **495 W ×253 D ×64 H mm;2.8 kg**. Large by the project's size bins. Pedal-up maximum height and cable clearance are additional/unverified. [G11-M p.104] |
| Simultaneous processing | Specification: **one amplifier plus nine effects**, DSP-limited. Operation pp.26,32 also describes up to ten freely combined effects; preserve that wording rather than assuming ten arbitrary high-load engines or unlimited duplicate amps. Overflow bypasses the excess effect. [G11-M] |
| Categories / choices | V2 effect list: Dynamics, Filter, Drive, Amp, Cabinet, Modulation, SFX, Delay, Reverb, Pedal, Send/Return and IR. The preserved2026-10-15 V2-list finding records **15 Dynamics choices**; remaining current per-category subtotals remain unverified. This historical count is not a reconciled installed-library census. V2 added one guitar amp, nine bass amps and57 bass effects; additional OverDrive, BitCrush and DynaDelay were distributed in2025–2026. Do not sum the site's overlapping/launch-era totals as a current installed inventory. [G11-E,G11-F,G11-P] |
| Presets / user memory | **500 editable patch memories,125 banks×4** in V2. This replaces the earlier240 total. Factory-filled subset is not established;500 is not500 factory plus500 user. The V2 release added200 supplied patches. [G11-M pp.4,8,104; G11-F] |
| Synth | **Yes:** Z-Syn, BaStdSyn and BaSynTlk bass-synth algorithms in the V2 SFX list. Their documented bass purpose is retained, not asserted to be unrestricted polyphonic guitar synthesis. [G11-E p.32] |
| Electric-to-acoustic simulation | **Yes: Aco.Sim**, specifically described as converting an electric guitar's tone toward acoustic guitar, in the Drive category. Not merely an acoustic amp or body IR. [G11-E p.9] |
| User IR loading | **300 total locations:001–106 supplied;107–300 available for user assignment**, hence **194 remaining user locations** by arithmetic. WAV,44.1–192kHz,up to2048 samples. This supersedes launch-page70 supplied+130 user figures. The source's remaining-user-location description is not a claim all factory IR slots are freely overwriteable. [G11-M pp.3,86–88;G11-F] |
| NAM / amp captures | Native NAM, converted NAM and user amp-capture playback/creation are **not established** in the checked official materials. Cabinet IR import alone proves none of them. |
| Instrument / aux | One6.35mm mono input,500kΩ; one3.5mm stereo AUX IN,10kΩ. No microphone input/phantom power in the full published connector list. [G11-M p.104] |
| Outputs / loops | Two6.35mm mono main L/MONO,R outputs; dedicated6.35mm stereo headphones; two6.35mm SEND and two RETURN sockets, usable as two mono loops or stereo send/return processing. INST/LINE output-level selection. No XLR output in the published list. [G11-M pp.13–14,77–79,104] |
| MIDI / USB | Separate five-pin DIN MIDI IN/OUT; USB-C DEVICE for audio/Guitar Lab; USB-A HOST for IR/update flash drives; dedicated optional-BTA-1 REMOTE connector. [G11-M] |
| Power | **12V DC,center-positive,2000mA AD-19 supply**. Supply rating, not measured operating draw. **USB bus power explicitly unsupported**. Internal battery operation is not documented. [G11-M p.104] |
| Editor platforms | **Guitar Lab Windows/macOS**; support page lists7.3 for Windows8.1/10/11 and macOS10.13–26. **Handy Guitar Lab iOS/iPadOS requires optional BTA-1**. Android/Linux full editor not established. Driver support is a separate field. [G11-D,G11-I] |
| USB audio | **4-in/4-out,44.1kHz/32-bit USB interface**; two dry and two processed recording channels, reamp. Internal A/D and D/A24-bit,processing32-bit. Windows ASIO driver; Mac/iOS/iPadOS do not require a dedicated driver. [G11-M pp.89–91,104] |
| Practice / stage | **Five-minute stereo looper**, independent of the effect slots;68 rhythm patterns;looper PRE/POST placement and rhythm synchronization;autosave and preset preselection. Do not infer a measured preset-change latency or global spillover guarantee. [G11-M,G11-P] |
| Firmware / status | Support page lists **system2.10** at this check. V2 memory changes are explicitly documented. ZOOM's UK page labels G11 discontinued; this does not prove no Indonesian retailer has remaining stock. [G11-D,G11-F,G11-UK] |

### G11 — official sources and inspection limits

[G11-P] [ZOOM global product](https://zoomcorp.com/en/us/multi-effects/guitar-effects/g11/). Some IR/preset copy is launch-era and is superseded by the dated V2 evidence.

[G11-M] [ZOOM V2 Operation Manual](https://zoomcorp.com/media/documents/E_G11v2.pdf), especially printed pp.3–4,8,11–14,23,26,32,86–91,104. Original text was read. Web screenshots of control/specification pages failed and direct PDF download failed; **no successful visual inspection is claimed**. Full rotary count and auxiliary-display properties remain unresolved accordingly.

[G11-E] [Official V2 Effects List](https://zoomcorp.com/media/documents/E_G11v2_FX-list.pdf), category contents and specifically read acoustic/synth entries. Screenshot failed; complete category-count transcription is still pending, not represented as completed.

[G11-F] [Official firmware2.0 announcement,2021-12-24](https://zoomcorp.com/en/gb/news/g11_updat/):240→500 patches;200→300 IR locations;added bass processing. This is a dated feature change, not a claim the owner's unit was updated.

[G11-D] [Official support/download page](https://zoomcorp.com/en/us/multi-effects/guitar-effects/g11/g11-support/), system2.10,drivers and editor versions as checked2026-10-15.

[G11-I] [Official Handy Guitar Lab announcement](https://zoomcorp.com/en/ca/news/zoom-announces-handy-gl-app-g11-available-app-store/), iOS/iPadOS editing and optional Bluetooth adapter requirement.

[G11-UK] [Official UK product/status page](https://zoomcorp.com/en/gb/multi-effects/guitar-effects/g11/).

Reviewed **2026-10-15**. Guitar/bass-capable multi-effects; **large**, built-in expression. Distinct from G6. Specifications use Zoom's model-specific manual and effect list; Shopee supplies only qualified hardware-price evidence. Partial record.

### Required comparison

| Field | Evidence-backed result |
|---|---|
| Expression | Built-in pedal with toe-operated on/off switch; one external FP02M CONTROL IN connection. External pedal is not included [M] |
| Knobs / footswitches / buttons | **27 / 11 / 1** from the manual's panel layout: six amp knobs, five four-knob effect sections, rear MASTER; five effect switches plus two each for scroll, patch and bank; one AMPLIFIER button. Toe and power switches separate. PDF panel rendering failed, so visual corroboration remains an inspection limitation [M] |
| Dimensions / weight | **495 W × 253 D × 64 H mm; 2.8 kg**. Pedal travel/cables require additional clearance [M] |
| Displays | **Seven displays:** main **5-inch TFT color touchscreen, 800×480**; six auxiliary **128×32 dot-matrix LCDs** (five effects, one amp). Auxiliary diagonals and explicit panel color-depth specifications unverified; do not call them six additional 5-inch screens [M,P] |
| Concurrent processing | **One amp plus nine effects, DSP-limited** in specifications; operation section describes up to ten combined processing items. Send/return consumes an effect position; large effects can span two physical control sections without proving a second independent algorithm [M] |
| Presets | **500 editable patch memories, 125 banks × four**. Saving/overwriting/deleting documented; factory-filled subset not established in the inspected current sources [M] |
| Acoustic simulator | **Yes: Aco.Sim**, explicitly electric guitar to acoustic-style sound [E] |
| Synth | **Yes:** Z-Syn, BaStdSyn and BaSynTlk in the V2 SFX library, with bass-use labels. Do not equate every pitch effect with synthesis [E] |
| IR import | **V2 manual: 300 locations, 106 supplied and 194 remaining (107–300)**; WAV, 44.1–192 kHz, up to2048 samples. Landing page's earlier70 supplied/130 user description is retained as older/conflicting product copy, not used to erase the V2 storage map [M,P] |
| NAM / captures | Native NAM, converted NAM and amp-capture loading **not established**. User cabinet IR import is separate |
| Power | **12 V, center-positive, 2 A AD-19 supply**; adapter rating, not measured draw. **USB bus power explicitly unsupported**. Internal battery operation not established [M] |
| Editing | **Guitar Lab Windows/macOS**; iPhone/iPad wireless editing with **optional BTA-1** and Handy Guitar Lab. Current support lists Guitar Lab7.3 and G11 firmware2.10. Android/Linux full editors not established [D,M] |
| USB audio | **4-in/4-out, 44.1 kHz/32-bit**; dry/wet recording and reamp. Windows ASIO driver; Mac/iOS class-compliant audio. Data connectivity does not provide unit power [M] |

### Effect categories and revision context

V2 effect list contains **12 categories**: dynamics, filter, drive, amp, cabinet, modulation, SFX, delay, reverb, pedal, send/return and IR. The dynamics section has **15 named choices**. The full updated counts for the other sections have not been reconciled with post-V2 downloadable additions; no unsupported total is supplied.

Zoom's product page advertises 22 cabinet emulations alongside older IR totals, while the V2 list includes added bass algorithms and cabinets. Its news lists67 added effects in December2021, OverDrive in December2025, BitCrush in February2026 and DynaDelay in April2026. Download availability is not proof of the installed library on a particular unit. The original manuals and current support version are recorded separately [P,E,D].

### Connections and practice

One 6.35 mm mono instrument input; **two mono FX loops** (two sends, two returns), combinable for a stereo external effect; 3.5 mm stereo aux; two mono 6.35 mm main outputs; **separate 6.35 mm stereo headphone output**. Unlike G6, headphones do not share the left main output [M].

Separate five-pin DIN MIDI IN/OUT, external expression CONTROL IN, USB-C DEVICE, USB-A HOST for flash-drive IR/firmware import, REMOTE adapter port and DC. No XLR microphone/phantom connection in the full inventory. Send/return placement and MIDI tempo/program transmission are configurable [M].

**Five-minute stereo looper**, pre/post placement, overdub/undo/redo and rhythm synchronization; **68 drum patterns**, tuner, autosave and silent preselection. Rhythm/looper functions are independent of the ordinary amp/effect slots [P,M].

### B1Xon — verified library and pedal behavior

Checked 2026-10-18 against the explicitly shared [100-effect list, ©2014 Z2I-2129-01](https://www.zoom.co.jp/sites/default/files/products/downloads/pdfs/E_B1on_B1Xon_FX-list_100.pdf), pp2–25, and [B1on/B1Xon manual](https://zoomcorp.com/media/documents/E_B1on_B1Xon.pdf), pp26–27.

| Field | Model-specific evidence / qualification |
|---|---|
| Processing / complete category census | **21 dynamics/filter +14 overdrive/distortion +12 amp +32 modulation/SFX +21 delay/reverb +5 PEDAL =105 entries**. The 25 asterisk-marked Ver1.2 additions reconcile the older **80** with **105**; installed firmware remains unverified. Cabinet selections are amp parameters, not extra slots. |
| Expression / five pedal algorithms | **PedalVx, PedalWah, PDL Reso, BaPDLPit, BaPDLMnP**. BaPDLMnP explicitly requires single-note playing. Pitch Color variations are parameter mappings, not extra algorithms. |
| Expression / assignment and switching | **AUTO ASSIGN can control multiple PEDAL effects together**. NO ASSIGN disables control; INPUT VOL controls input; OUTPUT VOL excludes rhythm/looper volume. The pedal switch toggles assigned effects, but **that on/off state is not saved**. This resolves the earlier toe-switch unknown. |


### B1Xon — synth choices and save/loop limits

Checked 2026-10-18 against the explicitly shared [effects list pp17–18](https://www.zoom.co.jp/sites/default/files/products/downloads/pdfs/E_B1on_B1Xon_FX-list_100.pdf) and [operation manual pp10–11,15,18–19,24–25,29](https://zoomcorp.com/media/documents/E_B1on_B1Xon.pdf).

| Field | Evidence / qualification |
|---|---|
| Synth / named algorithms and tracking | **Ba Synth, StdSyn, SynTlk, V−Syn, 4VoiceSyn, Z-Syn**; additionally **Z-Organ** and **Defret**. Ba Synth and 4VoiceSyn explicitly use single-note playing. StdSyn offers four variations; SynTlk four vowel pairs; Z-Syn saw/square waves. Variations are not separate algorithms; other algorithms’ polyphony and acoustic-guitar simulation remain unverified. |
| Memory / automatic saving and updates | **AUTO SAVE defaults ON**, retaining parameter edits as made. OFF prompts save/discard when changing patches; unsaved patches cannot be swapped. Firmware updating preserves saved patches, while All INITIALIZE resets defaults. Factory-filled count remains unverified. |
| Looper / time and clearing | Manual or **1–64 quarter notes within 30 seconds**; oversize settings adjust automatically. **Changing recording time or tempo erases the loop**. Tempo **40–250 BPM** is shared with effects/rhythms. HOME remains accessible during recording/playback. Undo and power-off persistence remain unverified. |
| Processing / DSP ceiling | **PROCESSING LIMIT bypasses an effect** when a five-effect combination exceeds capacity. Five slots do not guarantee every combination. |

### B1Xon — conversion, analog ratings and battery conditions, 2026-10-18

[Shared manufacturer manual, printed pp5–6,31](https://zoomcorp.com/media/documents/E_B1on_B1Xon.pdf), Z2I-1995-01. Shared electrical specifications apply to both named models; variant-specific size/effect counts remain separate.

| Field | Specification / qualification |
|---|---|
| Conversion / processing | **44.1kHz; 24-bit ADC and DAC, each128×oversampling; 32-bit floating-point and32-bit fixed-point processing**. These are internal formats, not USB recording or user capture specifications. |
| I/O / input and output ratings | INPUT **−20dBm rated,470kΩ**; AUX **−10dBm rated,1kΩ**. Shared stereo OUTPUT line maximum **+2dBm into≥10kΩ**; headphones **17+17mW into32Ω**. Manual defines0dBm=.775Vrms; published unit retained. Input ratings are not maximum headroom. |
| Power / runtime qualification | Approximately **20hours with four alkaline AA cells and LCD backlight OFF**. Battery-type setting improves remaining-charge indication; cold can shorten operation. The INPUT cable activates battery operation; AUX goes directly to OUTPUT without effects. No runtime inferred for NiMH,backlight-on operation or an aged used unit. |


### B1Xon — text-derived rotary and button census, 2026-10-18

| Field | Supported count / remaining gap |
|---|---|
| Knobs / footswitches / buttons | **1 / ? / 8**, derived from the [own shared manual, Part Names p4](https://zoomcorp.com/media/documents/E_B1on_B1Xon.pdf): one Parameter knob; HOME, MENU, ENTER, RHYTHM/LOOPER and four cursor keys (up/down/left/right). ENTER is separately labelled as a key; RHYTHM/LOOPER is one combined key. The extracted footswitch label gives no count, so its total remains unverified here. B1Xon’s pedal switch is additional. This supersedes the preserved all-unknown rotary/button entries; no visual panel audit claimed. |


## G6

Consolidated 2026-10-16. The complete original observation below retains its technical date, prices, sources, contradictions and unknowns. This is a document consolidation, not a fresh stock or firmware verification. Historical next-model notes are provenance; use research-state.json for current work.

<!-- ZOOM-G6 source begins -->

Reviewed **2026-10-15**. Guitar multi-effects; **large**, built-in expression. Specifications below come from Zoom's product, operation manual and effect list, not the Shopee description. Partial: exact current library subtotals and current market ranges remain unresolved.

### Required comparison

| Field | Verified result / limitation |
|---|---|
| Expression | Built-in pedal with toe-operated on/off switch; external FP02M through CONTROL IN [M] |
| Knobs / footswitches / separate buttons | **5 / 6 / 0** on accessible panel: four front parameter knobs plus rear MASTER; four effect switches, PLAY MODE and TAP. Pedal on/off and power switches are additional, not counted twice [M,E] |
| Dimensions / weight | **418 W × 228 D × 65 H mm; 1.94 kg**. Maximum expression travel and cable clearance additional [M] |
| Screen | **4.3-inch TFT color touchscreen, 480×272** [P,M] |
| Simultaneous processing | **Up to nine effect spaces, DSP-limited**. Large effects occupy two spaces; send/return also uses a space. The Japanese page describes nine effects or seven plus an amp. Do not treat the English landing page's abbreviated seven-effect line as the complete architecture [M,J] |
| Categories / choices | Published effect list has **12 categories**: dynamics, filter, drive, amp, cabinet, modulation, SFX, delay, reverb, pedal, send/return, IR. Complete current category counts remain unverified; do not mix the 2021 list with later Guitar Lab additions without labelling dates [E,P] |
| Memory | **240 editable patches, 60 banks × four**, including **100 factory-supplied presets**, not 240+100 [P,M] |
| Acoustic / synth | **Aco.Sim electric-to-acoustic simulator verified** in DRIVE category. No dedicated synthesizer established in the inspected list; SFX/pitch processing alone is not enough to mark synth yes [E] |
| IR | **70 factory IRs plus 130 user locations (071–200)**. Import WAV, 44.1–192 kHz, 16/24/32-bit through SD/Guitar Lab workflow. Maximum sample-length limitation not independently established [M] |
| NAM / captures | Native NAM, converted NAM and amp-capture import **not established**. IR loading is a separate capability |
| Audio I/O | Mono 6.35 mm input; mono send/return loop; 3.5 mm stereo aux; two 6.35 mm main outputs. **LEFT/MONO doubles as stereo headphone output**; there is not a separate headphone jack. SEND socket is listed as TRS, but loop operation is described as mono [M] |
| Other ports | External control, USB **Micro-B**, SD/SDHC/SDXC, REMOTE for optional BTA-1, DC. No XLR microphone/phantom or physical DIN/TRS MIDI connection in complete manual inventory [M] |
| Power | **9 V, 500 mA, center-negative AD-16 supply**. **USB bus power explicitly unsupported**. Battery operation not specified; 500 mA is the adapter requirement, not measured draw [M] |
| Apps | **Guitar Lab Windows/macOS** full effect/patch management and editing. **Handy Guitar Lab iOS/iPadOS requires optional BTA-1**. Android/Linux full editor not established. Current support lists Guitar Lab7.3 and G6 firmware2.00 [M,D] |
| USB audio | **2-in/2-out, 44.1 kHz/32-bit**; Windows ASIO driver, Mac/iOS class support. Interface compatibility does not establish USB-powered operation [M] |
| Practice/live | **90-second mono or 45-second stereo looper without SD**, up to two hours with SD, 256 loop files; **68 rhythm patterns**, tuner, preselection, patch/stomp/looper modes [M,P] |

### Price evidence in IDR

**Retail ask Rp6.319.000**, [Destiny Sound, Shopee shop10671590/item10498751255](https://shopee.co.id/Zoom-G6-Guitar-Multi-effects-Processor-i.10671590.10498751255). The actual item page reopened, but still contains **9 June delivery text** and a JavaScript-limited body. This is cached listing evidence, not live inventory, verified new condition or delivered price. **Used IDR: unknown**; no condition-verified used quote obtained. Warranty, bundle, stock and seller authentication are not inferred from a seller name or rating.

### Official sources, dates and remaining work

[P] [Zoom global/US product](https://zoomcorp.com/en/us/multi-effects/guitar-effects/g6/): screen, factory preset subset, IR overview, software and practice features. It announces OverDrive in December2025, BitCrush in February2026 and DynaDelay in April2026. These downloadable additions are not proof every existing unit has them installed.

[J] [Zoom Japanese product](https://zoomcorp.com/ja/jp/multi-effects/guitar-effects/g6/), explicit nine-effects/seven-plus-amp wording. Some regional pages mark the model discontinued; seller stock must still be checked separately.

[M] [Official operation manual v2](https://zoomcorp.com/media/documents/E_G6_v2.pdf): printed pp.11–13 controls/connectors, pp.36–38 looper, pp.47–49 DSP/large effects, pp.92–93 IR locations, p.112 specifications. Parsed text was available; attempts to render control/specification pages failed, so no successful visual inspection is claimed for those tables.

[E] [Official effects PDF](https://zoomcorp.com/media/documents/E_G6_FX-list_2.pdf): contents list and named Aco.Sim verified; PDF cover/front-panel illustration and printed p.7 successfully rendered. The 2021 appendix is not represented as a newly tested complete 2026 library.

[D] [Official support/downloads](https://zoomcorp.com/en/us/multi-effects/guitar-effects/g6/g6-support/).

Remaining: full firmware/library-matched category counts, IR length limit, exact newly available used/new offers and any undocumented capabilities. **Next Shopee-first model: Hotone Ampero II Stomp MP-300.**

LoopRoll samples/holds10–3000ms with footswitch latch/momentary options; it is distinct from the30-second phrase looper. HotSpice is sitar-tone simulation, not independent synth proof. | [Effect sheet p17][ZOOM-G1-FOUR-E] |

| Processing / Amp choices | **16 entries: 13 unstarred + 3 starred**. Unstarred: MS 800, MS 1959, FD TWNR, FD B-MAN, FD DLXR, UK 30A, BG MK1, XtasyBlue, HW 100, Recti ORG, ORG120, DZ DRV, MATCH30. | Processing / Cabinet choices | **16 entries: 13 unstarred + 3 starred**. Unstarred: MS4x12, MS4x12GB, FD2x12, FD-B4x10, FD-DX1x12, UK2x12, MK1 1x12, BGN4x12, HW4x12, RCT4x12, ORG4x12, DZ4x12F, MA2x12. Starred: MS4x12AL, FD MA2x12, MK3 1x12. | [Effect sheet pp12–14][ZOOM-G1-FOUR-E] |
| Processing / Modulation choices | **22 entries: 11 unstarred + 11 starred**. Unstarred: Tremolo, StereoCho, Phaser, VinFLNGR, TheVibe, Vibrato, Octave, PitchSHFT, HPS, CloneCho, CoronaTri. ### G2 FOUR — current manual processing and live-use clarification

Checked 2026-10-16 against the [manufacturer HTML operation manual](https://zoomcorp.com/manuals/g2four_g2xfour-en/), sections Replacing effects, Using the looper and Using audio interface functions. These expand the preserved 2026-10-14 observations above.

| Field | Additional verified limit |
|---|---|
| Processing restrictions |At most one amp, pedal, rhythm and looper category instance per patch; DSP overflow bypasses effects. Rhythm and looper are selectable effects, not unlimited extra blocks |
| Looper / rhythm |80 s mono or40 s stereo; enabling UNDO reduces limits to40/20 s. Minimum1.5 s; manual or1–64 quarter-note length, rhythm synchronization and count-in |
| Looper switching |Patch change stops recording/playback; entering the Library Screen stops and clears the loop. A tempo change can delete a synchronized/timed loop |
| USB audio routing |Two processed recording channels to computer/phone; two playback channels enter after effects. Windows requires the model driver; Mac/mobile do not. Playback is not documented as a reamp input through the chain |

The [current official product page](https://zoomcorp.com/en/us/multi-effects/guitar-effects/g2-four-g2x-four/) lists57 downloadable additions, including54 fromNovember2024 and later OverDrive,Modulation Delay2 andStereo Guitar Graphic EQ7. This is not a verified installed category total for every unit. No app or driver was installed.

### B1 FOUR — source qualifications and original context

Checked **2026-10-14**. Records are **partial**, not certifications that every field is complete. `?` = unverified, not No. Dimensions are **width × depth × height, mm**. `K/F/B` = rotary controls / footswitches / separate buttons; toe switches are additional. Prices below are **USD** seller observations, excluding destination tax/shipping. A missing used price is not estimated from the new price.

B1-family downloads now advertise additional BitCrush and DynaDelay releases in February and April 2026; these are downloadable library additions, not evidence that all installed units contain an identical count. On B6, the manual specifies **six** simultaneous effects; do not confuse the nine footswitches with nine processing slots.

[B1 manual] [Manufacturer operation manual](https://zoomcorp.com/documents/129/E_B1FOUR.pdf), originally checked at printed pp7 and60. The two small arrow buttons are additional to the two main footswitches.

[P1] [Thomann B6 cross-listed comparison](https://www.thomannmusic.com/zoom_b6.htm) and [B1 FOUR listing](https://www.thomannmusic.com/zoom_b1four_bass_multi_effect.htm): original $85 is a US-facing USD ask excluding destination tax/shipping, not IDR or a current sale.

[Official B1/B1X family product](https://zoomcorp.com/en/us/multi-effects/bass-effects/b1-four-b1x-four/). Source remains [effects/bass.md](ALL-BASS.md)<!-- historical-link:zoom-020 -->; old incoming links are unchanged.

### B1 FOUR — loop input, stopping and power settings

Checked 2026-10-18 against the [own operation manual](https://zoomcorp.com/media/documents/E_B1FOUR.pdf), printed pp9,35–38,48,50; browser text only.

| Field | Model-specific evidence / qualification |
|---|---|
| Looper / recording and stop behavior | **AUX IN is not recorded**. Rhythm playback enables quantization that adjusts loop timing; MEMORY, STOMP and EDIT remain accessible during loop playback. Stop choices are **Instant**, **Finish** (at loop end) and **Fade Out**. These settings do not establish loop persistence after power-off. |
| Power / battery and ECO | Battery-meter setting selects **ALKALI or Ni-MH** to match installed cells; this does not establish internal charging. ECO defaults ON and powers off after **10 hours without use**; OFF disables that function. |
| Display / backlight and contrast | Backlight choices: **OFF, ON, 15 seconds or 30 seconds after the last operation**. Contrast spans **1–13**. These settings do not resolve diagonal, color or touch capability. |

### B1X FOUR — pedal limits and analog specifications

Checked 2026-10-18 against the [own B1 FOUR/B1X FOUR operation manual Z2I-3612-01](https://zoomcorp.com/media/documents/E_B1FOUR.pdf), printed pp53–55,60. The pedal section explicitly applies to B1X FOUR; browser text only.

| Field | Evidence / qualification |
|---|---|
| Expression / assignment and toe state | **Only one PEDAL-category effect per patch**. Pressing the front of the pedal toggles that effect, but **does not save its on/off setting**. Six published pedal/control entries therefore do not mean six simultaneous pedal targets. |
| Expression / calibration and friction | Calibration follows the screen prompts; **Complete!** confirms success and **Error!** requires restarting. Button icons are absent from extracted text, so the startup shortcut remains unverified. A separate **5 mm hex screw** adjusts friction: clockwise tightens, counterclockwise loosens. Excessive loosening can disconnect it internally; this is not an electronic sensitivity adjustment. |
| I/O / analog conversion and levels | **44.1 kHz; 24-bit A/D and D/A, both 128× oversampling; 32-bit processing**. INPUT −20 dBu/470 kΩ; AUX −10 dBu/1 kΩ. Maximum line +2 dBu; manual wording “10 kΩ or more output impedance” retained. Headphones **17+17 mW into 32 Ω**. These converter specifications do not establish USB audio. |

### B2 FOUR — DI and bypass routing, 2026-10-17

[Manufacturer operation manual](https://zoomcorp.com/manuals/b2four-en/), ©2023, sections Selecting the DI MODEL and Using the bypass function; text inspection only.

| Field | Verified behavior / limits |
|---|---|
| DI / I/O routing | DI MODEL affects **BALANCED OUTPUT, OUTPUT and PHONES**. Its name does not mean it is restricted to the XLR output. This differs from the master-EQ/volume scope explicitly naming OUTPUT/PHONES above. |
| Bypass / live | **ALL BYPASS** bypasses the effects section and DI model. **EFX BYPASS** bypasses effects while retaining DI modeling. A press selects all bypass; holding selects effects bypass. Unlit means neither is bypassed. Bypass resets off at power-on. Analog/relay true-bypass circuitry is not established by these mode labels. |

### B2 FOUR — pedal-category and practice limits

| Field | Verified restriction / qualification |
|---|---|
| Expression / pedal category ceiling | **Only one effect per patch can come from the pedal category.** FP02M controls the parameter marked with the pedal icon for that effect. Previewing a selection in the Library auditions that effect alone; it is not proof of the completed chain’s sound. [Own operation manual, Setting pedal effects](https://zoomcorp.com/manuals/b2four-en/). |
| Looper / rhythm timing | Rhythm tempo **40–250**, shared with other effects and the looper; precount selectable and rhythm volume0–100. Rhythm continues when returning to MEMORY, but stops on patch change or Library entry. [Own rhythm instructions](https://zoomcorp.com/manuals/b2four-en/). |
| Live / tuner limits | Reference A **430–450Hz**. BASS tuning supports lowB–E–A–D–G–highC for five/six-string basses. Flat offsets1–3 semitones are available in BASS mode, not CHROMATIC. These settings are not a measured detection-accuracy specification. [Own tuner instructions](https://zoomcorp.com/manuals/b2four-en/). |

### B2 FOUR — overload and initialization behavior

Checked 2026-10-18 against the [manufacturer operation manual](https://zoomcorp.com/manuals/b2four-en/), Z2I-4836-01.

| Field | Verified behavior / qualification |
|---|---|
| Processing / DSP overflow behavior | When processing demand exceeds capacity, **PROCESS OVERFLOW** appears and the affected effect is bypassed. Five available positions therefore do not guarantee that every five-effect combination runs. |
| Memory / factory initialization | **ALL INIT overwrites all settings, including patch memories, with factory defaults.** This is not a settings-only reset. VERSION displays firmware, preset and BOOT versions separately; a preset version must not be reported as installed firmware. No reset or update was performed. |

### B2 FOUR — patch creation and recovery limits, 2026-10-18

[Own operation manual](https://zoomcorp.com/manuals/b2four-en/), Creating/Deleting/Reverting patch memories and Footswitch special functions. Text inspection; no device operations.

| Field | Behavior / qualification |
|---|---|
| Memory / empty-slot requirement | **NEW requires an empty patch memory**; its screen is unavailable when all300 are occupied. PATCH ERASE clears contents and marks the location **Empty**; it does not reduce the300-location capacity. |
| Memory / patch recovery scope | REVERT offers **PREVIOUS**, restoring the state immediately after selection, or **FACTORY**, restoring that patch’s factory state. These are patch-level operations, distinct from the documented all-settings ALL INIT reset; PREVIOUS is not proof of undo history across power-off. |
| Live / momentary effect control | Some effects can sound only while their footswitch is held; special-function parameters appear inverted. This is algorithm-specific behavior, not a universal momentary setting, extra footswitch or additional processing slot. |

Guitar Lab Windows/macOS and Handy Guitar Lab iOS/iPadOS with optional BTA-1 remain distinct from USB audio. Android full-editor support and current exact OS minimums were not established. No software,driver,firmware or dependency was installed.

### B6 — loop allocation and electrical limits

| Field | Verified detail / qualification |
|---|---|
| Processing / external loop allocation | Send/return consumes one of the six effect slots. Position saves per patch; FxLoop sends and returns at that position, while Send and Return modes expose the respective direction. DSP-overflow selection is rejected rather than automatically accepting an over-budget effect. [Official operation manual](https://zoomcorp.com/manuals/b6-en/). |
| I/O / analog levels | INPUT1/2 rated−20dBu, switchable1/10MΩ; RETURN rated−20dBu,1MΩ; AUX rated−10dBu,10kΩ. Main line and SEND maximum+11.4dBu at≥10kΩ load; phones24+24mW into32Ω. XLR impedance100Ω leg-to-ground/200Ω differential. [Official specifications](https://zoomcorp.com/manuals/b6-en/#specifications). |
| Processing / conversion | A/D and D/A24-bit,128×oversampling; internal processing32-bit. Response20Hz–20kHz,±0.5dB at10kΩ load. These differ from selectable USB transfer formats. [Official specifications](https://zoomcorp.com/manuals/b6-en/#specifications). |
| Editor / audio driver distinction | Windows requires B6 driver; Mac/iOS/iPadOS audio needs no driver. USB2.0 Micro-B data cable required; Lightning devices need a camera adapter. USB recording gain−6 to+6dB. This does not establish mobile editor compatibility without BTA-1. [Official interface instructions](https://zoomcorp.com/manuals/b6-en/). |

### V3 — effect parameter and bypass scope

Checked2026-10-18 against [official manual pp.10–14][V3-M],Z2I-3982-02;web text only,no files downloaded.

| Processing / per-effect adjustment | EFFECT ADJUST changes:Harmony original/harmony balance;TalkBox correction;Vocoder correction/octave;PitchCorrectKey andChromatic correction;Octave original/effect balance;Unison andChorus mix;Whistle drymix/octave;Distortion amount;Telephone frequencyband;BeatBox boost;Deep/Child/FormantCharacter voicecharacter;Robot pitch. FormantCharacter restores originalvoice atknobcenter. These are parameter functions,not selectable algorithm counts. |
| Expression / selected-effect bypass | FP02M duplicates EFFECT ADJUST;FS01 switches **selected effect** off. This doesnot establish all-chain bypass,mute,delay/reverb disable orpatch browsing. EFFECT OFF targets the selectedeffect;delay/reverb have separate knobs,fullyleft off. |
| Processing / Enhance | ENHANCE both emphasizes highfrequencies forclarity andsuppresses sibilance. COMP reduces above-threshold levelvariation;fullyleft disables it. No independently editable de-esser threshold isestablished. |
| Factory / user settings reset | Factory initialization overwrites **allsettings**,thenrestarts. This isnot evidence ofnamed patch-bank capacity. Procedure buttonicons aremissing fromweb text;no shortcut inferred orreset performed. |

| Power / battery types and USB connector | Own manualpp5,14 explicitly identifies **USB Micro-B** and alkaline,NiMH orlithium AA settings. Match the battery setting for accurate remaining-charge indication; no NiMH/lithium runtime or in-unit charging capability inferred. [Own manual][V3-M] |

### G3Xn — Auto Save and eco behavior

Checked 2026-10-18 from indexed manufacturer-authored [shared G3n/G3Xn manual](https://zoomcorp.com/media/documents/E_G3n_G3Xn.pdf), printed pp25–26. Direct PDF reader rejected its14MB size; indexed exact-document passages were available. No file downloaded.

| Field | Documented behavior / qualification |
|---|---|
| Memory / Auto Save off | SETUP→AUTO SAVE supports ON/OFF. With OFF, patch/effect parameters are not saved automatically. Changing patches after edits prompts for saving: **YES saves before changing;NO discards changes**. The manual points to p19’s save procedure. This resolves the previous unknown, without claiming automatic recovery after power loss. |
| Power / eco shutdown | With eco enabled, the unit switches off after **10 hours without use**. This is inactivity shutdown, not a battery-runtime or current-consumption specification. |


### G3Xn — overflow and looper edit limits

Checked 2026-10-18 from indexed [original shared manual](https://zoomcorp.com/media/documents/E_G3n_G3Xn.pdf), printed pp9,39. Exact-document passages, not another Zoom model’s behavior.

| Field | Evidence / qualification |
|---|---|
| Processing / overflow behavior | Exceeding processing capability **or unit count** displays **PROCESS OVERFLOW** and bypasses effects. The manual directs deleting effects or changing their types to end bypassing. Seven available positions therefore do not promise every combination; the existing100-point chart remains separately revision-qualified. |
| Looper / recording-time changes | MANUAL or1–64quarter-note lengths are offered. Settings beyond available recording duration adjust automatically. **Changing recording time deletes recorded data**; this is separate from the already-recorded patch-change and tempo-change erasure rules. |
| Looper / undo scope and layout | With UNDO enabled, only the latest overdub is undone; REDO restores it. The manual distinguishes a1second hold on the1-unit looper’s control from two presses on the first unit’s control for the2-unit looper. Extracted control icons remain unverified; do not assign a physical FS number from text alone. |

Mic On/Off here describes cabinet microphone emulation, not evidence of a physical mic input. G3Xn port inventory still requires exact-model corroboration. |
| Editor / clipboard scope | The shared Guitar Lab section documents copying **up to four effects with custom parameters** onto other patches. This is software clipboard capacity, not four additional onboard processing slots, patch locations or snapshots. Device firmware/library applicability remains separately qualified. |


### G3Xn — tuner access and output choice

Checked 2026-10-18 from indexed [original manual pp28,30](https://zoomcorp.com/media/documents/E_G3n_G3Xn.pdf), explicitly covering G3n/G3Xn.

| Field | Documented operation / limits |
|---|---|
| Live / tuner access | Hold MEMORY/STOMP in either STOMP or MEMORY mode, then choose **FS1 for bypass sound** or **FS2 for muted output** while tuning. These are selectable tuner output behaviors, not evidence of relay true bypass or retained delay/reverb tails. |
| Controls / tuner settings menu | MENU→TUNER opens TUNER SETTINGS through the PUSH TO ENTER control. The indexed p30 screen shows calibration, tuner type and flat-tuning settings. An example440Hz display alone does not prove the complete range; the own-product page below resolves the range. Power-cycle persistence remains unverified. |


### G3Xn — flat-tuning limits

Checked 2026-10-18 from indexed [original G3n/G3Xn manual p33](https://zoomcorp.com/documents/140/E_G3n_G3Xn.pdf).

| Field | Exact-document evidence / gaps |
|---|---|
| Live / flat tuning | TUNER SETTINGS→FLAT supports **one,two or three semitones down**. Flat tuning is **unavailable with TYPE=CHROMATIC**. This changes the tuner reference targets, not an audio pitch-shift effect or added synth capability. The own-product page below resolves the calibration range; power-cycle persistence remains unverified. G3/G3X manual values are not assigned to G3Xn. |

### Remaining checks

Remaining: full footswitch/button totals and visual corroboration; display diagonal/color/touch; revision-matched category choices/DSP costs; initial/current factory-memory allocation; complete synth inventory; USB subtype/control wiring and phantom specification; IR/NAM/capture import; battery/USB supply; USB channels/routing; looper block cost; qualified used/new condition and checkout stock. Bass suitability remains unclassified. No sibling specifications substituted.

### V2 manual text follow-up

Checked2026-10-17 through manufacturer-hosted indexed text (printed pp.4,44,50); no PDF or image download/visual inspection. The named controls include FS1–FS4,TUNER,BANK/SCROLL,mode and TAP; the pedal switch is separately named. Full physical K/F/B remains pending rather than inferred from incomplete text diagrams; toe operation is now established by the V2 pedal instruction linked above.

The USB quantization and looper limits above supersede the original unknown fields; they do not establish current firmware architecture, import capabilities or live seller stock.

### Firmware and DSP revision qualifications

[Official firmware history](https://zoomcorp.com/documents/3435/G5n_System_Firmware_Version_History_ver300_en.pdf):V2.00 (February2017) optimized amp processing,added the two-unit looper and fixed concurrent rhythm/looper noise and recording failures. V3.00 (April2018) added Guitar Lab3 support,tuner/encoder improvements,OutputVP with volume carryover between patches,and effects previously distributed online. These notes do not prove audio-tail carryover or identical DSP cost between versions.

The [V3 chart](https://zoomcorp.com/documents/1326/E_G5n_G3n_G3Xn_PowerChart_v2.pdf) supplies the100-point budget; the separately labelled [older V2 chart](https://www.zoom.co.jp/sites/default/files/products/downloads/pdfs/E_G5n-V2.0_FX-power-chart.pdf) supplies the looper costs above. Neither establishes per-category counts or an arbitrary nine-effect combination. Checked2026-10-17 through official indexed text only; no file downloaded or firmware executed.

### Acoustic and synth evidence scope

Checked2026-10-17 through official indexed text. The exact G5n V2 patch list establishes model applicability of Aco.Sim; the shared G5n/G3n/G3Xn parameter sheet supplies its controls. Patch052 Gayageum combines Aco.Sim with PitchSHFT; that is another composite preset,not a second independent acoustic-simulation engine. Aco.Sim models electric-to-acoustic tone and does not establish pickup-specific acoustic body IR loading.

Exact-model USB-channel and signal-flow searches did not establish channel count,loopback or reamping. Search results for the older G5 and other Zoom devices were excluded. These fields stay unverified; no negative capability conclusion follows from unsuccessful indexed searches.

### G5n — memory and external-pedal operation, V2 manual

Checked 2026-10-18 using the [manufacturer-hosted V2 operation manual](https://zoomcorp.com/media/documents/E_G5n-V2.0.pdf), original text extraction only; no local download, execution or visual inspection.

| Field | Evidence / qualification |
|---|---|
| Memory / save behavior | Printed pp21–22: SAVE chooses a destination and requires confirmation; SWAP exchanges two patches (p23). AUTO SAVE OFF disables automatic patch/effect writes and prompts on changing an edited patch: YES saves, NO discards (p28). This does not establish initial factory filling of all200 locations. |
| Expression / external assignment | EXT PDL enables two pedal effects; the second selected pedal effect is assigned to the external pedal (p29). This is not two arbitrary expression destinations or evidence of an external toe switch. |
| Memory / initialization versus firmware | All Initialize restores every setting and patch to factory defaults (p52); updating firmware preserves saved patches (p55). The extracted reset-start button icon is missing, so no button sequence is inferred. |

These resolve operation details,not current-firmware factory allocation or the remaining panel,display,import,power and price gaps above.


### G5n — rotary count and analog specifications, V2

Checked 2026-10-18 from original text in the [manufacturer V2 manual](https://zoomcorp.com/media/documents/E_G5n-V2.0.pdf); no local download or visual inspection.

| Field | Evidence / qualification |
|---|---|
| K/F/B / rotary subtotal | **20 rotary controls**, derived from four units ×four parameter knobs (p3),plus CONTROL,MASTER LEVEL,BOOST,TONE (pp4–5). Encoder press is not another separate button. Full footswitch/button totals remain unverified. |
| I/O / analog ratings | P58: mono INPUT **−20 dBu nominal,470 kΩ**; stereo mini AUX **−10 dBu nominal,10 kΩ**; two mono outputs **+5 dBu maximum into≥10 kΩ**; stereo mini phones **20+20 mW into32 Ω**. Nominal input levels are not maximum-input limits;0 dBu=.775 V. |
| I/O / rear inventory | Pp4–5 list INPUT,AUX IN,OUTPUT L/R,PHONES,CONTROL IN,USB,DC9V and POWER. No separate MIDI,XLR or audio send/return is listed. USB subtype and control wiring remain unverified; USB audio does not prove USB power. |
| Power / operating behavior | P6: connect AC adapter before POWER ON; last-used operating mode returns. ECO shuts down after10 hours unused. This does not establish battery or USB-powered operation. |

Earlier unknown rows remain dated provenance; these details supersede their rotary-count and analog-rating gaps only.


### G5n — USB monitoring, display adjustment and preselection

Checked 2026-10-18 against original text in the [manufacturer V2 manual](https://zoomcorp.com/media/documents/E_G5n-V2.0.pdf), printed pp25–26,30,32. No download, execution or visual inspection.

| Field | Evidence / qualification |
|---|---|
| USB audio / recording and monitoring | RECORDING GAIN adjusts the level sent to the computer from **−6 to +6 dB**. MONITOR BALANCE spans **0–100**: 0 selects direct input only; 100 selects computer playback only. These controls do not establish channel count, dry recording, loopback or reamping. |
| Display / contrast | Each selected display has contrast adjustment **1–13**. Contrast adjustment does not establish screen diagonal, color or touch capability. |
| Live / bank preselection | With PRESEL enabled, changing banks changes the displayed selection until a patch switch confirms the change. This is selection staging, not evidence of scenes, seamless switching or audio-tail retention. Missing button icons in extracted instructions are not reconstructed. |


### G5n — audio-software compatibility evidence

Checked 2026-10-18 from original text in Zoom’s [July 2025 compatibility chart](https://zoomcorp.com/media/documents/EFX_DAW_compatibility_2025JUL_E.pdf), linked by [G5n support](https://zoomcorp.com/en/us/multi-effects/guitar-effects/zoom-g5n/g5n-support/). This is a dated manufacturer test matrix, not fresh operation testing.

| Field | Evidence / qualification |
|---|---|
| USB audio / tested Mac software | G5n is marked compatible with **Cubase Pro14, Logic Pro11 and GarageBand10.4 on macOS Sequoia15**; and **Cubase Pro13, Logic Pro10.8 and GarageBand10.4 on macOS Sonoma14**. These are specific tested combinations, not universal DAW or later-OS certification. |
| Editor / driver distinction | DAW compatibility is separate from Guitar Lab patch/effect management. The shared driver installation guide uses LiveTrak L-8 as its example; its Mac instructions do not establish that G5n requires a Mac driver. The chart omits G5n from its iOS/iPadOS rows; omission does not prove incompatibility. Windows marks are not assigned to applications here because extracted column ordering is ambiguous. |

USB channel count, AUX-to-recording routing, dry recording, loopback and reamping remain unverified by the primary sources inspected. Third-party forum reports and unrelated Zoom models are not promoted to manufacturer specifications. No file or software downloaded.


### G5n — pedal toggle state and tempo synchronization

| Field | Manufacturer V2 manual evidence / qualification |
|---|---|
| Expression / toe-toggle setting distinction | **Toe-down toggles the pedal effect but does not change its ON/OFF setting**, whereas the separately described button operation does change that setting. The extracted button icon is absent, so its physical identity is not inferred. This distinction does not establish an external toe switch or universal momentary operation. [Own manual p46](https://zoomcorp.com/media/documents/E_G5n-V2.0.pdf). |
| Live / tempo synchronization prerequisite | Tempo serves the looper, rhythm and delay, plus some modulation effects. Effect synchronization requires a compatible effect and its Time/Rate parameter set to a **musical-note value**; tapping alone does not make every effect tempo-synchronized. [Own manual p47](https://zoomcorp.com/media/documents/E_G5n-V2.0.pdf). |

### G5n — looper persistence and unit usage

| Field | Manufacturer V2 manual evidence / qualification |
|---|---|
| Looper / instances and units | Four choices: **one-unit mono, two-unit mono, one-unit stereo, two-unit stereo**; **only one LOOPER per patch**. Two units describe interface/effect-unit occupation, not two independently running loopers. DSP cost remains separately version-qualified. [Own manual p41](https://zoomcorp.com/media/documents/E_G5n-V2.0.pdf). |
| Looper / phrase lifetime and quantization | Switching to MEMORY mode preserves recording/playback. **Changing patches stops and deletes the phrase**. With rhythm playing, recording follows the precount and its ending is quantized. **Changing rhythm tempo during loop playback erases the phrase**. This is not persistent loop storage. [Own manual p42](https://zoomcorp.com/media/documents/E_G5n-V2.0.pdf). |
| Drums / mode and patch changes | Rhythm continues across STOMP/MEMORY mode changes but **stops on patch change**. Tempo spans **40–250 bpm**, volume **0–100**; shared tempo does not override the effect synchronization prerequisite above. [Own manual pp39–40](https://zoomcorp.com/media/documents/E_G5n-V2.0.pdf). |

### B1on — conversion, synth tracking and delay tails

Checked 2026-10-18; original manufacturer documents read as browser text, without file downloads.

| Field | Evidence / qualification |
|---|---|
| I/O / conversion and USB scope | [Own manual p31](https://zoomcorp.com/media/documents/E_B1on_B1Xon.pdf): **44.1 kHz; 24-bit A/D and D/A, both 128× oversampling; 32-bit floating-point and fixed-point processing**. AUX rated −10 dBm/1 kΩ; line +2 dBm maximum into ≥10 kΩ. USB is listed for **firmware update**; bus power is separately explicit on p6. This does not establish USB audio, MIDI, connector subtype or an editor. |
| Synth / additional single-note algorithm | [100-effect list p17](https://www.zoom.co.jp/sites/default/files/products/downloads/pdfs/E_B1on_B1Xon_FX-list_100.pdf) also names **4VoiceSyn**, marked as a **Ver1.2 addition** by the list legend. It adds synth harmony to **single notes**, with nine Mode choices and two Scale variations. These are parameters within one algorithm, not 18 additional effects or polyphonic tracking proof. |
| Looper / delay tail distinction | Same effects list p19: Delay reaches **4000 ms**; TapeEcho **2000 ms**. Tail ON preserves decay when the individual effect is disabled; it does not establish tails across patch changes or loop persistence. |


### B1on — complete published category census

Checked 2026-10-18 against [manufacturer 100-effect list, ©2014, Z2I-2129-01](https://www.zoom.co.jp/sites/default/files/products/downloads/pdfs/E_B1on_B1Xon_FX-list_100.pdf), pp2–25. Counts enumerate named entries, not parameter variations.

| Field | Published inventory / qualification |
|---|---|
| Processing / categories | **Dynamics/filter 21; overdrive/distortion 14; amp simulator 12; modulation/SFX 32; delay/reverb 21: total 100 non-PEDAL entries.** Five simultaneous slots remain a separate DSP-limited ceiling. |
| Processing / revision accounting | The legend marks **Ver1.2 additions** with an asterisk: category subtotals **4/3/0/11/7 = 25 additions**. Removing these yields **17/11/12/21/14 = 75**, reconciling the original manual’s 75 with this 100-entry library. This is document accounting, not an installed-firmware audit or a claim that firmware1.30 introduced the additions. |
| Processing / cabinet and pedal scope | Appendix lists **12 cabinet parameter choices plus OFF** within amp models; these do not add standalone algorithms. The separate **five PEDAL entries** belong to B1Xon and are excluded from B1on’s 100. |

Original guitar multi-effects, distinct from G3X and G3n. Checked 2026-10-17; partial. Discontinued. Bass/vocal suitability unclassified. Later shared-manual evidence below is not proof of the offered unit’s installed firmware.

| Expression / controls K/F/B | External FP01/FP02 expression or FS01 footswitch via CONTROL IN. Built-in pedal belongs to G3X; G3 toe N/A. Three main footswitches; complete knob/button counts pending panel verification. | [Manual][G3-M] pp4–5; [Product][G3-P] |
| External control assignments | Per-patch TOTAL settings. FP01/FP02 controls volume/effect parameters; OUTPUT VOL excludes rhythm/looper level. Manual min/max can invert response; AUTO ASSIGN can control several supported effects together and fixes their ranges. FS01 supports effect-specific functions, bypass/mute, tap tempo or no assignment; assigned effect must be on. | [Current official manual][G3-MC] pp16–19 |
| Display | Three backlit dot-matrix LCDs; each display has a contrast adjustment. Backlight dimming can reduce consumption. Published diagonal, color depth and touch remain unverified. | [Product][G3-P]; [current manual][G3-MC] pp22–23 |
| Dimensions / weight | 234 W ×170 D ×54 H mm; 1.2 kg excluding batteries. Large footprint. | [Japanese specifications][G3-J] |
| Simultaneous effects | Up to six with firmware **2.00 or later** (June2012), freely ordered, DSP-limited; overflow bypasses an effect. Off effects still consume DSP. Multiple amp models simultaneously are prohibited per FAQ. | [Manual][G3-M] p10; [FAQ][G3-Q]; [Firmware history][G3-F] |
| Categories / choices | 116 total: 94 effects plus22 amp/cab models in later product description. Version2.00 added amp modeling and an Auto Save ON/OFF setting. Version2.10 (May2013) fixed a rhythm hi-hat/tempo issue and added Windows8/USB3.0 support. Category subtotals and earlier library counts unresolved; USB3.0 host support does not establish USB3 transfer speed. | [Product][G3-P]; [Firmware history][G3-F] |
| Presets / memory | 100 editable memories (10×10), factory-filled; not100 factory plus100 additional user slots. Auto Save and Store/Swap. | [Product][G3-P] |
| Synth / acoustic | MonoSynth detects single-note pitch; Aco.Sim transforms electric-guitar tone toward acoustic. Separate algorithms, not an acoustic preamp claim. | [Manual][G3-M] pp45,52 |
| I/O | Quarter-inch mono INPUT with active/passive selection; mono R and stereo L/MONO/PHONES outputs; balanced XLR with PRE/POST and ground lift; CONTROL IN, USB and DC. USB/control connector subtype pending. | [Product][G3-P]; [Manual specifications][G3-S] |
| Power / battery / USB | AD-16: 9V DC center-negative,500mA adapter rating; four AA, approximately 6 h alkaline; Ni-MH also supported. Set the battery type correctly for accurate remaining-charge indication. USB bus power is supported. ECO enters standby after about 25 minutes of inactivity; incoming guitar signal prevents automatic standby. Measured draw and USB electrical requirements remain unknown. | [Manual specifications][G3-S]; [FAQ][G3-Q]; [current manual][G3-MC] pp2,6,23 |
| USB audio / MIDI | 2-in/2-out stereo,16-bit44.1kHz. REC GAIN adjustable; playback exits through G3 outputs. Monitor balance0–100 mixes post-effects direct sound with DAW return;100 monitors DAW only. USB MIDI and dry/reamp paths unverified. | [FAQ][G3-Q]; [Manual][G3-M] pp24–25,36 |
| Routing / output target | Global signal-flow direction is reversible. DIRECT targets headphones/monitors; COMBO/STACK FRONT target amp inputs; COMBO/STACK POWER AMP target returns. XLR PRE supplies the original signal before effects, while main outputs remain processed. No free parallel routing established. | [Current official manual][G3-MC] pp5,21–22 |
| IR / NAM / capture | User IR, native/converted NAM and capture imports unestablished. Factory cab modeling is not import evidence. | Unresolved |
| Editor / platforms | Edit&Share patch editor/librarian: Windows XP/Vista/7/8/8.1/10, Mac10.9–10.12 listed. Firmware2.10 updaters and Windows ASIO2.0.1 driver are separate; current OS compatibility unknown. | [Support][G3-D] |
| Looper / rhythms / live | 40s, or20s with Undo enabled; overdub/erase. Time/tempo changes and power-off erase loop; returning Home does not. 41 rhythms with looper synchronization. Undo/redo affects the last overdub; stop modes are immediate, end-of-loop or fade-out. Tuner modes include chromatic, seven-string guitar, Open A/D/E/G and DADGAD. Non-chromatic modes support dropping by one to three semitones. Scenes/trails remain unresolved. | [Manual][G3-M] pp30–35; [Product][G3-P]; [current manual][G3-MC] p27 |

| Categories / choices | **111 numbered types: 99 non-amp effects plus 12 amps.** Manual census, grouped here by function rather than official category labels: dynamics/noise **8 (001–008)**; EQ/filter **14 (009–022)**; boost/drive/preamp **15 (023–037)**; amps **12 (038–049)**; modulation/pitch/special **24 (050–073)**; synth/organ **7 (074–080)**; fretless simulation **1 (081)**; delay **12 (082–093)**; reverb/ambience **8 (094–101)**; combined effects **5 (102–106)**; pedal effects **5 (107–111)**. These sum to 111, not simultaneous blocks. Cabinet Table1 has 12 named cabinets plus ORGN automatic selection within amp parameters; do not add another 12 to the numbered total. | [Product][B3-P]; [Manual][B3-M] pp33–49; checked2026-10-18 |
| Factory / user memory | 100 editable memories (10×10), first50 factory-filled; not150 total. Parameter changes save automatically;Store/Swap copies or exchanges with another destination,the current patch cannot be its own destination. All Initialize restores patches/settings;firmware update preserves saved patches. | [Product][B3-P]; [Manual][B3-M] pp12–13,30–31 |
| Synth / acoustic | Seven synth/organ types: MonoSyn, StdSyn, SynTlk, V-Syn, 4VoiceSyn, Z-Syn and Z-Organ (074–080). MonoSyn detects single-note pitch; 4VoiceSyn adds harmony to single notes with nine modes and two scale variations per mode, not polyphonic input tracking. StdSyn has four sound variations. Defret (081) simulates fretless bass; this does not establish upright/acoustic-body conversion. Dedicated acoustic/upright simulation remains unverified; Acoustic (043) models the Acoustic 360 amplifier. | [Manual][B3-M] pp39,44–45,49; [Product][B3-P]; checked2026-10-18 |
| I/O | Mono quarter-inch INPUT with active/passive switch; mono R and stereo L/MONO/PHONES; XLR balanced PRE/POST and ground lift; CONTROL IN,USB,DC. USB/control subtype unknown. | [Product][B3-P]; [Specifications][B3-S] |
| I/O / input and DI switches | ACTIVE (pressed in) is specified for an active-pickup bass or an effect pedal preceding the B3; PASSIVE (out) for a directly connected passive bass. The manual describes impedance selection but gives no separate ACTIVE impedance. XLR GROUND LIFT (in) disconnects the grounding pin; CONNECT (out) connects it. PRE/POST selects the XLR signal before/after effects. | [Manual][B3-M] pp4–5; checked2026-10-18 |
| Analog levels / conversion | INPUT −20dBm,1MΩ; R and L line maximum +5dBm into ≥10kΩ; headphones20+20mW into32Ω. Balanced XLR impedance100Ω hot/ground or cold/ground,200Ω hot/cold. A/D and D/A24-bit,128×oversampling,44.1kHz; distinct from16-bit USB audio. | [Manual][B3-M] p51 |
| Power / battery / USB | AD-16 rating9V DC500mA center-negative;fourAA, approximately6h alkaline; USB bus power supported. Adapter rating is not measured draw; USB electrical requirements unknown. USB bus operation requires POWER switch OFF before connection; standby disables audio input/output. | [Specifications][B3-S]; [Manual][B3-M] pp32,50 |
| USB audio / MIDI | 2-in/2-out;16-bit44.1kHz. Recording gain ±6dB; monitor balance0–100 mixes processed direct sound with DAW return (0 direct-only,100 DAW-only). For monitoring through DAW software, the manual specifies 100: other settings mix direct and computer returns and can produce a flanger-like sound. USB MIDI/dry/reamp routing unverified. | [Product][B3-P]; [Manual][B3-M] pp20,32; checked2026-10-18 |
| External control / routing | External-pedal OUTPUT VOL excludes rhythm/looper levels. Manual min/max can invert response. FS01: bypass/mute,tap tempo or effect function; assigned effect must be on. Global signal-flow direction reversible. Per-patch TOTAL level0–120 and original/effected balance0–100 (0 original-only,100 effected-only);master level is global. | [Manual][B3-M] pp14–19 |
| Tuner / bypass | Chromatic or bass tuner; A4 reference 435–445 Hz. Drop tuning is unavailable in chromatic mode. A one-second tuner activation bypasses effects; two seconds mutes output. The switch icon is missing from extracted text, so the exact physical activation control remains unverified. In eco mode, about 25 minutes without use enters standby; a bass input signal prevents standby. This is not loop-storage or charging evidence. | [Manual][B3-M] pp6,22–23; checked2026-10-18 |
| Battery / backlight settings | Select ALKALINE or Ni-MH for accurate remaining-charge indication;this does not establish onboard charging. Backlight dim delay1–30s orON;LCD contrast adjustable separately for each display. | [Manual][B3-M] pp19–21 |
| IR / NAM / capture | User IR,native/converted NAM and capture imports unestablished. Factory cabinets do not prove import capability. | Unresolved |
| Editor / platforms | Edit&Share Windows XP/Vista/7/8/8.1/10 and Mac10.9–10.12. Firmware1.20 updaters and Windows ASIO2.0.1 driver are separate. Driver listing explicitly names Windows7/8/8.1/10. Rechecked manufacturer support2026-10-17; it does not establish Windows11 or newer macOS support. Mobile compatibility unknown. | [Support][B3-D] |
| Looper / rhythms / live | 40s maximum,20s with Undo enabled; overdub/erase and rhythm synchronization. Changing TIME or tempo and switching power off erase the loop; Home screens preserve it. Undo/redo affects last overdub. Stop modes: immediate,end-of-loop,fade-out. Rhythm recording uses precount and quantization. 41 rhythm-list entries:GUIDE1,36 patterns2–37,and four metronomes38–41;not41 drum kits. Tempo40–250. Scenes,trails and exact switch gestures unresolved. | [Manual][B3-M] pp26–29,50; [Product][B3-P] |
| Firmware / known issues | History Z2I-2238-01:1.00 production November2011;1.10 May2013 fixes rhythm hi-hat/tempo and Z-Syn silence with excessive input, adds Windows8/USB3.0 host support. Version1.20 September2014 fixes output muting during continuous MonoPitch Shift operation. Offered unit version unknown; host compatibility does not establish USB3 transfer speed. | [Firmware history][B3-F] |
| Processing / additional published ratings | Manufacturer lists **32-bit** processing, **120 dB input-conversion S/N**, and **−100 dBm residual noise floor**. Its level footnote defines 0 dBm as0.775 Vrms; preserve the manufacturer's notation rather than silently rewriting units. These are published ratings, not independent measurements. | [Product][B3-P]; checked2026-10-18 |
| Looper / recording format and patch-change claim | Product explicitly describes loop recording as **16-bit/44.1 kHz**. Its feature list claims **1 ms patch changes**; this is a manufacturer claim and does not establish delay/reverb spillover, uninterrupted arbitrary patch switching or a measured local latency. | [Product][B3-P]; checked2026-10-18 |
| Analog frequency-response conflict | Frequency response: product +1/−6dB versus directly inspected manual +1/−3dB,20Hz–20kHz at10kΩ. Preserve discrepancy; no measured resolution. | [Product][B3-P]; [Specifications][B3-S] |

### B3 — Edit&Share editing and initialization behavior, 2026-10-18

[Original Zoom software guide](https://zoomcorp.com/media/documents/EditShare_Operation_Manual_English_2.pdf),revisionEdit&Share-Manual-E-5,linked by [B3 own support page](https://zoomcorp.com/en/us/multi-effects/bass-effects/b3/b3-support/). |---|---|

No app was run orpatch modified. |

Original guitar multi-effects; distinct from G5n. Manufacturer marks discontinued. Checked2026-10-17; partial. Bass/vocal suitability and ownership unestablished.

| Field | Finding / qualification | Evidence |
|---|---|---|
| Expression | Built-in three-dimensional Z-Pedal; external FP01/FP02 or FS01 via control input. The reproduced manual states the vertical pedal direction can switch an effect on/off; exact toe-switch mechanics remain unverified. | [Product][ZG5-P]; [Indexed manual][ZG5-M]; [Reproduced manufacturer manual p13][ZG5-R] |
| K/F/B | **14 rotary controls** derived from four sections×three parameter knobs plus BOOST/TONE. Four numbered effect footswitches; pedal and booster switching are additional. Complete footswitch/button totals and visual corroboration pending. | [Product][ZG5-P]; [Manual p34][ZG5-M]; [Reproduced parts p4][ZG5-R4] |
| Display | Four backlit LCDs. Diagonal, graphics color and touch unverified. | [Product][ZG5-P]; [Indexed specifications][ZG5-M] |
| Dimensions / mass | 190 D ×470 W ×90 H mm;3.1kg. | [Indexed specifications][ZG5-M] |
| Processing / category choices | Up to9: eight regular effects plus one Z-Pedal effect. Product advertises123 effects plus22 amp/cabinet models; manual specifications say125 types plus Z-Pedal effects. Reproduced manufacturer manual p63 instead states145 types, arithmetically matching123+22 but not proving identical revision/content. Regular versus Z-Pedal counting is clarified below; product-total arithmetic is explained below; full functional subtotals and edition identity remain unresolved. | [Product][ZG5-P]; [Indexed manual introduction/specifications][ZG5-M]; [Reproduced specifications][ZG5-R63] |
| Categories / regular and Z-Pedal census | Reproduced manual regular list ends at **125 PDL MnPit** (p57); separate Z-Pedal list runs **#1–#20** (pp59–61). Thus **125+20=145 listed entries**, explaining the p63 total versus indexed “125 plus Z-Pedal” wording. The 20 include **Custom**, a mapping mode for other effects, so 145 is not a claim of 145 unique audio algorithms. Cabinet Table1 has **22 named cabinets plus OFF** as amp parameters, not extra regular-numbered blocks. Product “123 effects+22 amp/cab models” is arithmetically explained in the next row; revision equivalence is not proven by arithmetic. | [Reproduced manual p57][ZG5-R57], [p58][ZG5-R58], [pp59–61][ZG5-R59]; checked2026-10-18 |
| Categories / product-total reconciliation | Manufacturer-manual text on a second mirror identifies **22 amp entries 043–064** (FD COMBO through REVO-1), followed by Tremolo065. Arithmetic: **125 regular −22 amps =103 regular non-amp entries; 103+20 Z-Pedal =123; 123+22=145**. This provides a consistent counting explanation for the product wording, not proof of identical firmware editions or 145 distinct algorithms. Cabinet choices remain parameters. Second mirror matches the existing reproduction’s numbering, Z-Pedal list, 297 patches and G5 architecture; site FAQs are excluded. | [Manufacturer manual reproduction pp45–49][ZG5-R2]; checked2026-10-18 |
| Processing / overload | DSP FULL bypasses **all effects**; disabled effects still consume the same processing allocation. Change types or select THRU to reduce demand. Do not inherit G3/G3X overflow behavior. | [Manufacturer manual reproduction p10][ZG5-R2]; checked2026-10-18 |
| Factory / user memory | 297 patches,99banks×3; factory-filled/blank split unresolved. Auto-save switch added in firmware1.10. | [Indexed manual][ZG5-M]; [History][ZG5-H] |
| Synth / acoustic | Reproduced manual lists **042 Aco.Sim**, electric-guitar acoustic timbre with Top/Body controls; **089 MonoSynth**, single-note input-pitch synthesis with Sine/Tri/SawUp/SawDn waveforms; and **090 Z-Organ**, organ simulation. These verify distinct acoustic/synth functions, not polyphonic synthesis or external capture import. Numbering belongs to this reproduced edition; existing total-count conflict remains unresolved. | [Reproduced manufacturer manual p45][ZG5-R45], [p53][ZG5-R53]; checked2026-10-18 |
| I/O | Mono input1MΩ with active/passive selection; two mono L/R outputs; stereo phones; balanced XLR with PRE/POST and ground lift; control input; USB audio. Full connector/control inventory pending. | [Indexed specifications][ZG5-M] |
| I/O / electrical ratings | Reproduced manufacturer specifications: rated input −20 dBm, 1 MΩ; separate ACTIVE-mode impedance unspecified. L/R line maximum +5 dBm into ≥10 kΩ. Dedicated stereo phones: 20+20 mW into 32 Ω. XLR impedance 100 Ω hot/ground or cold/ground, 200 Ω hot/cold. A/D and D/A are 24-bit with 128× oversampling at 44.1 kHz; internal processing uses 32-bit floating and fixed point. Frequency response 20 Hz–20 kHz, +1/−3 dB at 10 kΩ. Converter resolution does not establish USB stream bit depth. | [Reproduced manufacturer manual p63][ZG5-R63]; checked2026-10-18 |
| Power / battery / USB | AD-16:9VDC center-negative,500mA supply rating, not measured draw. Battery/USB bus-power operation unverified. | [Indexed specifications][ZG5-M] |
| Power / eco and pedal adjustment | Eco automatically turns power off after 10 hours of non-use; ON keeps it on. Manual startup specifies connecting AD-16 before switching on, without establishing battery or USB-bus operation. Z-Pedal vertical and horizontal torque adjust separately using a 5 mm hex key: side screw for vertical, bottom screw for horizontal; clockwise tightens. Calibration is documented, but extracted control icons and six movement diagrams are missing, so exact calibration gestures remain unverified. | [Manufacturer manual reproduction pp7,38–39][ZG5-R2]; checked2026-10-18 |
| IR / NAM / capture | User IR, native/converted NAM and capture imports unverified; factory amp/cab modeling does not establish import support. | Unresolved |
| USB audio / monitoring | **16-bit, 44.1 kHz**, explicitly in the reproduced audio-interface chapter; distinct from 24-bit A/D and D/A. Direct/DAW balance and recording level adjustable; set USB AUDIO MONITOR to 100 when monitoring through DAW software to avoid mixed/flanger-like output. Historical interface OS list: XP SP3 32-bit, Vista SP1 32/64-bit, Windows7 32/64-bit; Intel Mac OS X10.5/10.6/10.7. This is separate from Edit&Share compatibility and does not establish current OS support, channel count, USB MIDI or bus power. | [Reproduced manufacturer manual p35][ZG5-R35]; checked2026-10-18 |
| Editor / software | Edit&Share editor/librarian: Windows XP/Vista/7/8/8.1/10; MacOSX10.9–10.12. Separate G-Series ASIO2.0.1 driver lists Windows7/8/8.1/10. Firmware1.20 Windows/Mac listed. Current OS compatibility unverified. | [Support][ZG5-S] |
| Looper / drums / live | Rhythms, chromatic tuner and12AX7 tube booster. Looper up to60s with rhythm synchronization; **UNDO ON reduces maximum to30s**, with last-overdub undo/redo by holding the switch1s. STOP ends immediately; FINISH ends after the loop; FADE OUT fades, with another press stopping immediately. Tube booster offers0–16dB, may be silent~5s while warming, starts OFF and cannot amplify USB-recorded signals. Scenes/trails unresolved. | [Product][ZG5-P]; [Indexed manual pp32,34][ZG5-M]; [Reproduced manual p33][ZG5-R33] |
