## G3X

Original guitar multi-effects with expression; distinct from G3 and G3Xn. Manufacturer marks discontinued. Checked2026-10-17; partial. Bass/vocal suitability and ownership unestablished.

| Field | Finding / qualification | Evidence |
|---|---|---|
| Expression | Built-in expression; pedal switch toggles its assigned effect. **External FP01/FP02 volume-pedal use on G3X is explicit on p17**. Page18 assigns external real-time effect-parameter control to G3 and built-in control to G3X; broader external parameter control on G3X remains unverified. Adjustable min/max can reverse response; AUTO ASSIGN fixes endpoints. Exact toe mechanics unverified. | [Product][ZG3X-P]; [Shared manual pp17–19,59][ZG3X-M] |
| Expression / calibration and resistance | Printed p37 documents sensitivity calibration for weak or excessively abrupt pedal response; an ERROR result requires restarting calibration. Mechanical resistance is separately adjustable with a **5mm hex key** at the side screw: clockwise tightens, counterclockwise loosens. Excessive loosening can release the screw inside the unit. This does not establish toe-switch construction or external-pedal calibration support. | [Manufacturer manual p37][ZG3X-M]; checked2026-10-18 |
| K/F/B | Nine rotary parameter controls derived from three sections×three; three main footswitches plus pedal switch. Complete separate-button count and visual corroboration pending. | [Manual pp4–5][ZG3X-M] |
| Display | Three **backlit LCDs with contrast control**, confirmed by the archived exact-model manufacturer page. Diagonal, color and touch remain unverified. | [Product][ZG3X-P]; [Archived manufacturer page][ZG3X-OLD]; [Manual][ZG3X-M] |
| Dimensions / mass | **175 D ×323 W ×66 H mm;1.6kg**, explicitly G3X. | [Manual p59][ZG3X-M] |
| Processing / category choices | **Six simultaneous**, DSP-limited: overload bypasses an effect. Bypassed effects retain their processing allocation; change types or use THRU to avoid DSP Full. **116 types**, matching product94effects+22amp/cabinet models. Functional census follows below. | [Product][ZG3X-P]; [Manual pp3,10,58–59][ZG3X-M] |
| Categories / numbered census | Catalogue functional grouping, not official category names: dynamics/noise **7 (001–007)**; EQ/filter **10 (008–017)**; boost/drive/preamp **20 (018–037)**; acoustic simulation **1 (038)**; amps **22 (039–060)**; modulation/pitch/special **22 (061–082)**; synth/organ **2 (083–084)**; delay **12 (085–096)**; reverb/ambience **8 (097–104)**; combined effects **7 (105–111)**; pedal effects **5 (112–116)**. Sum **116**, including **94 non-amp effects**. DynaDelay 091 on p40 is an explanatory example, not another type. Cabinet Table1 lists 22 named cabinets plus OFF inside amp parameters, not 22 additional numbered blocks. | [Manual pp40–57][ZG3X-M]; checked2026-10-18 |
| Factory / user memory | **100 writable patches;10banks×10**. The appended G3/G3X factory patch list contains **100 named entries**, matching the entire documented user capacity: the archived manufacturer prose also explicitly says all100 user memories are pre-loaded with factory settings. Initial fill is therefore directly corroborated, with no additional protected100-slot bank established. Current contents of a used unit and firmware-specific factory-list changes remain unverified. AUTO SAVE ON stores edits automatically; OFF requires manual saving. Store/Swap copies or exchanges patches; with AUTO SAVE ON, the active patch cannot be its own destination. Firmware updates preserve saved patches; All Initialize restores all settings, including patches, to factory defaults. | [Manual pp3,14–15,24,38–39,59 and appended Patch List][ZG3X-M]; [Archived manufacturer page][ZG3X-OLD]; checked2026-10-18 |
| Synth / acoustic | **083 MonoSynth** detects input pitch for single-note guitar synthesis; **084 Z-Organ** simulates organ. **038 Aco.Sim** converts electric-guitar timbre toward acoustic. These are separate effects, not proof of polyphonic synthesis. | [Manual pp45,52][ZG3X-M] |
| I/O | Mono input1MΩ with active/passive selection; R output; shared L/MONO/PHONES; balanced XLR with PRE/POST and ground lift; control input,USB,DC9V. | [Manual pp4–5,59][ZG3X-M] |
| I/O / electrical ratings | Rated input −20 dBm, 1 MΩ; separate ACTIVE-mode impedance unspecified. R and L line maximum +5 dBm into ≥10 kΩ; shared L/MONO/PHONES headphone output 20+20 mW into 32 Ω. XLR impedance 100 Ω hot/ground or cold/ground, 200 Ω hot/cold. A/D and D/A are 24-bit with 128× oversampling at 44.1 kHz; processing uses 32-bit floating and fixed point. Analog response 20 Hz–20 kHz, +1/−3 dB at 10 kΩ. These converter ratings do not change the 16-bit USB format. | [Shared manual p59][ZG3X-M]; checked2026-10-18 |
| Power / battery / USB | AD-16:9VDC center-negative,500mA supply,not measured draw. FourAA alkaline~6h. USB bus power supported; set POWER OFF before USB connection. | [Manual pp36,58–59][ZG3X-M] |
| IR / NAM / capture | User IR, native/converted NAM and capture imports unverified; factory models do not establish import support. | Unresolved |
| USB audio / monitoring | **2-in/2-out USB audio**, explicitly stated by the archived manufacturer page; **16-bit,44.1kHz** in the manual. USB channel-source mapping is not established by the channel count. Adjustable recording level and direct/computer monitoring balance; set USB AUDIO MONITOR to100 for DAW-only monitoring to avoid doubled/flanged sound. Legacy interface OS list differs from editor/driver support; current OS compatibility unverified. | [Manual pp24–25,36][ZG3X-M]; [Archived manufacturer page][ZG3X-OLD] |
| Editor / software | Edit&Share: Windows XP/Vista/7/8/8.1/10; MacOSX10.9–10.12. Separate ASIO2.0.1 driver: Windows7/8/8.1/10. Firmware2.10 Windows/Mac listed. Current OS compatibility unverified. | [Support][ZG3X-S] |
| Routing / global settings | OUTPUT choices: DIRECT for headphones/monitors, COMBO FRONT or STACK FRONT for amp inputs, COMBO POWER AMP or STACK POWER AMP for returns. Global signal-flow display direction is selectable. Patch level and global master level each span 0–120. Backlight dims after 1–30 seconds or stays ON; each LCD has separate contrast. ALKALINE/Ni-MH selection calibrates remaining-charge indication, not charging. | [Manual pp16,20–24][ZG3X-M]; checked2026-10-18 |
| Expression / assignments | Built-in INPUT VOL controls input level; OUTPUT VOL leaves rhythm and looper volume unaffected. AUTO ASSIGN can control all eligible active effects together. Optional footswitch assignments include bypass/mute, tap tempo and no assignment; effect-specific functions require the relevant effect ON. The G3X external FP01/FP02 volume function is distinct from these footswitch assignments. | [Manual pp17–18][ZG3X-M]; checked2026-10-18 |
| Looper / tuner and tempo | Tuner reference **435–445Hz**, retained across power-off; BYPASS or MUTE. Besides chromatic, string modes cover standard guitar including seven strings, Open A/D/E/G and DADGAD. Drop offsets of one to three semitones apply to string modes, **not chromatic**. Master tempo **40–250BPM** is shared by effects, rhythms and looper. Tuner presets do not establish bass processing suitability. | [Manual pp21,26–28][ZG3X-M]; checked2026-10-18 |
| USB audio / monitor and record levels | Monitor balance0–100 blends **post-effects direct signal** with DAW return:0 is direct only,100 computer only. USB recording-level trim is **±6dB**. This defines monitoring and trim,not independent dry/wet USB channels or a reamp input. | [Manual pp24–25][ZG3X-M]; checked2026-10-18 |
| Looper / drums / live | **1.5–40s;20s with UNDO**; rhythm synchronization. Changing duration/tempo or powering off erases loop. Last-overdub undo/redo uses a one-second hold. STOP/FINISH/FADE OUT stop modes; holding stop can interrupt the latter two immediately. **41 rhythm-list entries**, including guide/metronome patterns, not41 drum kits. Two-switch bank changes can briefly trigger the earlier switch and affect sound. Scenes/trails unverified. | [Manual pp13,30–35,59][ZG3X-M] |

### G3X — XLR source selection and eco standby

Checked 2026-10-18 against [manufacturer G3/G3X manual,printed5–6](https://zoomcorp.com/media/documents/G3_G3X_operationManual_English.pdf),linked by the G3X support page. These are shared rear-panel instructions; the external-volume/model distinction is clarified in the Expression row. No download or device operation.

| Field | Exact source qualification | Evidence |
|---|---|---|
| I/O / XLR source and ground | PRE selects signal **before effects**;POST selects **after effects**. GROUND LIFT separates the signal path from the grounding pin;CONNECT joins them. Neither setting establishes phantom-power support. | [Manual p5][ZG3X-M] |
| Power / eco standby | POWER’s **eco** setting enters standby after about**25 minutes** without use. An incoming guitar signal prevents this automatic standby. This is an optional switch setting,not an unconditional timeout or evidence that USB-powered operation follows identical switching behavior. | [Manual p6][ZG3X-M] |

### G3X — Edit&Share patch library and compatibility limits

Checked2026-10-18 against the [manufacturer editor manual](https://zoomcorp.com/media/documents/EditShare_Operation_Manual_English_2.pdf),13pages,Edit&Share-Manual-E-5,linked by the exact G3X support page. Read as browser text;no application,firmware or document downloaded locally.

| Field | Exact source qualification | Evidence |
|---|---|---|
| Editor / patch library | Editor changes effect types,on/off and parameters,renames/stores patches,and imports/exports patches and patch groups. Updating a device patch uses **Store**;updating a device group uses **Replace**. This is patch-data management,not proof of IR,NAM or capture import. | [Editor manual pp2,4–10](https://zoomcorp.com/media/documents/EditShare_Operation_Manual_English_2.pdf) |
| Memory / older patch compatibility | Manual explicitly includes **G3X** as a destination for **G3 firmware1.12-or-earlier patches and patch groups**. Reverse import and G5→G3X compatibility are not established. Its separate delay-clamping warning specifically names G3v2-or-later;do not silently apply that warning’s numeric table to G3X. | [Editor manual pp7,10,12](https://zoomcorp.com/media/documents/EditShare_Operation_Manual_English_2.pdf) |
| Editor / sleep and filtering | Edit&Share does not support computer sleep;manufacturer advises restarting it after sleep-related unresponsiveness. Effect filtering matches patches with that effect **ON**,and edits appear in results only after saving. Modern OS support remains unverified. | [Editor manual pp11–12](https://zoomcorp.com/media/documents/EditShare_Operation_Manual_English_2.pdf) |

## G2.1u

Original guitar multi-effects; distinct from G2.1Nu. Discontinued. Checked2026-10-17; partial. Ownership and bass/vocal suitability unestablished.

| Field | Finding / qualification | Evidence |
|---|---|---|
| Expression | Built-in; CONTROL IN:FS01/FP01/FP02 in parts text,FP02/FS01 in specifications. Full depression toggles the PEDAL ASSIGN module; assignment is saved per patch. External FP01/FP02 always controls volume separately per p21, in play/edit modes; connect before power-on. Generic parameter-table legend p26 instead describes FP01/FP02 effect-parameter control; retain this wording conflict. Built-in pedal controls volume in bypass. | [Manual pp18–21,35][ZG21-M] |
| K/F/B | **4 rotary /2 main footswitches /6 buttons**: module selector+three parameters; BANK−/+,STORE,TAP,PEDAL ASSIGN,RHYTHM. Power/pedal switching additional; visual corroboration pending. | [Manual pp6–7][ZG21-M] |
| Display | Two-digit seven-segment LED plus parameter/pedal indicators. Diagonal/color/touch unverified. | [Manual p35][ZG21-M] |
| Dimensions / mass | **165 D ×255 W ×79 H mm;1100g without batteries**. | [Manual p35][ZG21-M] |
| Processing / category choices | **54 effects;max9 simultaneous modules**. DRIVE offers16 distortion/amp choices plus1acoustic simulator. Each has live/direct modeling variants selected by CABINET & MIC state; do not count34 independently assignable DRIVE slots. Module selection counts below sum54; OFF and parameter/live-direct variants excluded. | [Manual pp29,35][ZG21-M] |
| Category choices / module inventory | **COMP1;WAH/EFX9;ZNR3;DRIVE17;EQ1;EXTRA EQ/CABI&MIC2;MOD/SFX13;DELAY3;REVERB5 —54 selections**. One active choice per module,not54 simultaneous blocks. Extra EQ and cabinet/mic are alternatives. | [Manual pp27–33][ZG21-M] |
| Category choices / routing and time | WAH/EFX POSITION permits before DRIVE or after EQ/EXTRA EQ on supported types. MOD/SFX delay/tape/dynamic-delay choices reach2s; dedicated DELAY/ECHO reach5s,with ping-pong alternative and FS01 hold. REVERB includes multi-tap delay up to3s. Cabinet follows DRIVE type; dynamic/condenser mic and3positions are parameters,not extra algorithms. | [Manual pp27–33][ZG21-M] |
| Factory / user memory | **80 slots:40 user+40 read-only presets**. Factory user area A0–d9 duplicates preset area00–39; All Initialize overwrites user edits with these originals. This resolves the earlier factory-fill gap; actual seller-unit contents unknown. | [Manual pp16,24,35][ZG21-M] |
| Synth / acoustic | ACOUSTIC converts electric-guitar timbre toward acoustic. Dedicated synth architecture unverified; a SynthDaze preset name alone is insufficient. | [Manual p29 and patch list][ZG21-M] |
| I/O | Mono input1MΩ;shared stereo OUTPUT/PHONES;CONTROL IN,USB,DC9V. USB audio16-bit stereo,44.1/48kHz;not internal96kHz processing. | [Manual pp6–7,35][ZG21-M] |
| I/O / conversion and analog ratings | Internal sampling **96 kHz**; A/D **24-bit, 64× oversampling**, D/A **24-bit, 128×**, processing **32-bit**. Response 20 Hz–40 kHz, +1/−3 dB at 10 kΩ. Rated input −20 dBm, 1 MΩ. Shared stereo line/phones output maximum +5 dBm into ≥10 kΩ, or 20+20 mW into 32 Ω. These are separate from the USB rates and 16-bit stream. | [Manual p35][ZG21-M]; checked2026-10-18 |
| Expression / response curves | UP spans minimum to maximum; DOWN reverses that range. HIGH spans the patch value to maximum; LOW spans minimum to the patch value. Thus HIGH/LOW use the stored patch value as an endpoint, rather than both being full-range sweeps. External-pedal wording conflict remains above. | [Manual p34][ZG21-M]; checked2026-10-18 |
| Power / battery / USB | AD-0006:9VDC,300mA,center-negative supply≠draw. FourAA alkaline~7.5h;USB bus power with POWER OFF. No battery charging. | [Manual pp2,35;USB guide][ZG21-M] |
| IR / NAM / capture | User IR, native/converted NAM and capture imports unverified. | Unresolved |
| Editor / software | Support lists Windows G-Series ASIO2.0.0 driver; desktop/mobile editor unverified. Bundled DAW/audio driver is not patch-editor evidence. | [Support][ZG21-S] |
| Looper / drums / live | **40 rhythm patterns;40–250 BPM**, available in play/bypass/mute; rhythm playback disables REVERB. Level0–30;tap tempo requires at least3 presses. Looper,scenes,trails unverified. | [Manual pp12–13,35][ZG21-M] |

Delayhold requires a supportingtype and repeats thecurrentdelay until asecondpress cancels hold; this doesnot establish a general recordinglooper. COMP/ISO2;ZNR/Gate2;DRIVE20distortion+2acoustic variants;EQ1;CAB7;MOD11;REV/DLY11. Counts/parameters do not reconcile automatically to50. | [Manual pp17–24][GFX3-M] |

No hardware calibration performed. | [Manual p16][GFX3-M]; checked2026-10-18 |
| EQ / Energizer and global levels | ENERGIZE is global, OFF or1–30: lower values emphasize treble, higher values bass. It remains active in bypass. MASTER LEVEL0–30 applies across patches; PATCH LEVEL1–30 is the final patch level. These controls are not evidence of additional independently selectable effect algorithms. | [Manual pp17,24][GFX3-M]; checked2026-10-18 |

Shared stereo line/headphone output maximum **+3 dBm into ≥10 kΩ**; headphone mW/load rating unspecified. Do not substitute GFX-5/G3 converter or output ratings. | [Manual p24][GFX3-M]; checked2026-10-18 |

Preserve that apparent duplication as a source issue; actual MIDDLE center frequency remains unresolved. | [Manual p19][GFX3-M]; checked2026-10-18 |
| Power / battery / USB | **9VDC center-negative,AD0006 300mA supply**,not draw. FourAA alkaline~11h;no recharging. USB power unverified. | [Manual pp2,24][GFX3-M] |

Category count is a manual-derived reconciliation,not physical firmware verification: eleven DRIVE positions each have three CLASSIC/STANDARD/MODERN choices;the first ten provide30amp/drive variants andACOUSTIC adds3. TOTAL is explicitly not an independent effect module;ARRM waveforms are control settings. The74headline agrees with this counting method,while the DRIVE prose and introduction use inconsistent terminology. Retain those qualifications.

Hold delay and sampler are distinct facilities,not a six-second overdub looper with all effects available. Guitar sampling is post-effects;AUX sampling pre-effects. Full-depression control can temporarily activate an off target. Energize also affects bypass but excludes AUX input (printedp7). No GFX-3/G5 specifications transferred. Official PDF has19webpages with paired printedpages;references use printed numbering. Only web text read;no files downloaded or physical inspection.

### Control-count follow-up

Printedpp4–5 name all21buttons;the nine rotary total includes the rear LEVEL control. Neither LEDs nor footswitches are counted as buttons. Printedp9 explicitly calls the expression mechanism a push-down switch. Screenshot attempts returned no readable image;this remains a manual-text inventory,not a visual or physical panel inspection.


### Expression routing and ARRM limits

| Field | Finding / qualification | Evidence |
|---|---|---|
| Expression / target replacement | Selecting WAH forces MOD to P-WAH; selecting PITCH forces MOD to P-PIT, replacing its previous type. These are not additional simultaneous blocks. GAIN controls DRIVE gain or, for some types, RESONANCE/TOP. ARRM+PEDAL varies modulation depth. | [Manual pp20–21][GFX5-M] |
| Processing / ARRM source conflict | Page21 diagrams eight waveforms, but page34 lists R-WAVE 1–9. No ninth waveform is identified; actual selectable count remains unresolved. R-SYNC lists eighth, quarter, half and dotted-half notes plus one–four measures. Both explanatory tables use 0.5 for eighth notes, whereas the page34 range heading says 0–3. Preserve that discrepancy. | [Manual pp21–22,34][GFX5-M] |
| Expression / pedal ranges | UP spans minimum→maximum; dn reverses it. Hi spans the stored patch value→maximum; Lo spans minimum→stored patch value. For ARRM+PEDAL these ranges govern modulation amplitude. | [Manual p22][GFX5-M] |

### Converter and electrical ratings

| Field | Finding / qualification | Evidence |
|---|---|---|
| Processing / converters and sampler | Internal sampling is **31.25 kHz**; A/D is **20-bit, 64× oversampling**, D/A **20-bit, 8× oversampling**. The separate sampler records/plays **16-bit linear** audio; this is not the converter resolution or a USB format. | [Manual p35][GFX5-M] |
| I/O / electrical ratings | Guitar and AUX rated inputs are both **−20 dBm**, with respective impedances **470 kΩ / 20 kΩ**. Line maximum is **+3 dBm into ≥10 kΩ**. Headphones are rated **50 mW into 32 Ω**; the manual does not identify that figure as per-channel. Its stated reference is 0 dBm = 0.775 Vrms. | [Manual p35][GFX5-M] |

### GFX-5 — pedal calibration and user-patch reset, 2026-10-18

[Zoom own manual](https://zoomcorp.com/media/documents/E_GFX5.pdf), printed pp26–27; document procedure only, no hardware operation performed.

| Field | Documented behavior / qualification |
|---|---|
| Expression / calibration and switching point | Power on holding ASSIGN: MIN. With pedal fully raised, press STORE: MAX. Depress pedal, release it, then press STORE to finish. The chosen forward position also sets the target-module on/off point; a somewhat higher position allows lighter activation. ERROR requires repeating from the raised-pedal step. This is a documented calibration-dependent switching point, not a separately published numeric toe-pressure threshold. |
| Memory / factory reset | Power on holding STORE: ALINIT; another STORE restores patches to factory defaults and returns to play mode. EDIT/EXIT cancels before confirmation. The section specifically describes restoring user-group patches; it does not establish reset behavior for pedal calibration, all global settings, or an external backup mechanism. Existing60user/60preset counts remain unchanged. |

### GFX-4 hold delay and patch continuation

Checked 2026-10-18 against official manual text; no downloads or physical test. This resolves the earlier hold-delay/continuation unknown, not a general overdubbing-looper claim.

| Field | Finding / qualification | Evidence |
|---|---|---|
| Looper / hold delay | PP-DLY or ECHO with FB/HLD=Hd or HS supports phrase capture in manual mode. Hold footswitch3 to record; releasing it or reaching the selected delay time starts repeat playback. Press again to stop. Delay range reaches 2 seconds; overdubbing is not established here. | [Own manual p27][GFX4-M] |
| Looper / seamless restrictions | Delay continuation requires both patches to have REV/DLY ON, PP-DLY or ECHO, and delay time at most 1 second. With both FB/HLD settings HS, a held phrase can continue after returning to program mode and changing patch. Bypass forces it to end. This is conditional continuation, not unrestricted spillover. | [Own manual p27][GFX4-M] |
| Expression / wah and pitch shortcut | The shortcut selects and enables PDLWAH or PDLPIT in MODULATION and changes EP-MOD from off to UP. It replaces the MOD type rather than adding a separate block. For these two effects, Hi behaves as UP and Lo as dn. | [Own manual pp20,26][GFX4-M] |

Combined stereo line/phones maximum **+4 dBm** into ≥10 kΩ; headphone mW unspecified. Reference: 0 dBm = 0.775 Vrms. | [Manual p29][GFX4-M] |
| Factory / reset | All Initialize restores every setting and overwrites user patches. Power on holding STORE; confirm with STORE, or cancel with MANUAL/CANCEL. These are documented instructions, not operations performed. | [Manual p28][GFX4-M] |
| Expression / calibration | Power on holding PEDAL; at MIN, raise pedal and press STORE. At MAX, fully depress, release foot to allow slight return, then STORE. | [Manual p28][GFX4-M] |

### G7.1ut — AUX recording, monitoring and editor connection

| USB / direct monitoring | USB+DIRECT retains processed guitar at OUTPUT alongside DAW playback; USB Only mutes that local processed-guitar feed while connected. Save the setting with STORE/SWAP. Disable DAW echo-back when retaining direct output to avoid doubled/flanged monitoring. [Own manual pp48–49][G71-M] |
| Editor / required connection | Editor/librarian patch storage,editing andreturn transfer require a computer **MIDI interface** and connections between the computer andunit’s **MIDI IN/OUT**. USB audio alone does not establish editor communication. Current OS support remains unverified. [Own manual p50][G71-M] |

All Initialize restores factory patches and overwrites edits. Energizer settings are not patch-stored. | [Manual pp27–29][G71-M] |

### 707II — card storage and sampler limits

| Field | Manufacturer manual evidence / qualification |
|---|---|
| Memory / SmartMedia groups | **Up to five patch groups and 60 samples per card**, manual p28. ALSAVE stores the entire USER group; external card storage does not increase the 120 onboard patch slots. [Own manual][707-M] |
| Looper / sampler persistence and routing | **pp14–15:** GT records the effected guitar signal; AUX bypasses effects. Card samples save automatically. A–B loop points reset when changing sample number or toggling sampler mode. **Sampler and rhythm cannot operate together**; MOD/REV are disabled during sampling. No larger-card recording duration inferred. [Own manual][707-M] |
| Looper / sampler playback modes | **p14:** normal; half speed/half pitch; half speed/original pitch; quarter speed/quarter pitch; quarter speed/original pitch. These are sample playback modes, not five additional effect algorithms. [Own manual][707-M] |

### 707II — expression assignment and calibration

| Field | Manufacturer manual evidence / qualification |
|---|---|
| Expression / per-patch range | **pp23–25:** PDLMIN and PDLMAX each span **0–10**, stored per patch; MIN greater than MAX reverses the sweep. Assigning WAH or PITCH temporarily replaces the current MODULATION type with P-WAH or P-PIT. Storing an assigned effect records its module as ON. [Own manual][707-M] |
| Expression / toe-switch exception | Toe press toggles the assigned effect or sampler. **VOL assignment has no toe-switch action**; physical toe-switch presence does not imply volume bypass. [Own manual pp24–25][707-M] |
| Expression / calibration | **p25:** power up holding PEDAL; at MIN, raise pedal and press STORE/EXECUTE; at MAX, press fully down, release foot, then press STORE/EXECUTE. That position determines the on/off threshold; a higher position allows lighter switching. ERROR requires repeating from MIN. Hardware operation was not performed. [Own manual][707-M] |

### 707II — converters and live patch selection

| Field | Manufacturer manual evidence / qualification |
|---|---|
| Processing / converters and sampler | **31.25 kHz** sampling; **20-bit A/D, 64× oversampling**, **20-bit D/A, 128× oversampling**. Sampler uses **16-bit linear** recording/playback, separately from converter resolution. [Manual p39][707-M] |
| I/O / electrical ratings | Guitar nominal **−20 dBm, 470 kΩ**; AUX **−20 dBm, 20 kΩ**. Combined stereo line/phones maximum **+3 dBm into ≥10 kΩ**; headphone mW unspecified. Reference: 0 dBm = 0.775 Vrms. [Manual p39][707-M] |
| Memory / selective factory recall | All Initialize restores all USER patches; Factory Recall restores a selected USER patch. Either overwrites affected edits. Both start by powering on holding STORE/EXECUTE; CANCEL/EXIT cancels before confirmation. This is documentation, not a reset performed. [Manual p32][707-M] |
| Live / bypass, mute and tuner | Briefly pressing/releasing both footswitches gives **dry bypass with pedal volume control**; holding both for **at least one second** then releasing mutes OUTPUT/PHONES. Tuner reference **435–445 Hz**, returning to **440 Hz after power-on**. Bypass is not an automatic mute. [Own manual p11][707-M] |
| Live / rhythm bank-selection limit | During rhythm playback, **direct bank selection is unavailable**; ordinary patch switching remains possible. USER-only, PRESET-only or both-group selection is available through the TYPE-key combination. [Own manual p10][707-M] |
| Routing / patch preselect | Preselect permits browsing while the current sound remains active; both footswitches together confirm the change. Power cycling restores immediate selection. This is not a spillover claim. [Manual p23][707-M] |
<!-- comparison evidence ends -->

### 707II — provenance and remaining work

[Manufacturer product page][707-P] explicitly identifies discontinued707II. [Own English manual][707-M],40pages: controls pp.4–5; memory/pedal pp.8–9; specification p.39. No revision date established from inspected sections. Source counts distinguish stored algorithms from concurrent modules and sample memory from patch memory. Fifteen front keys count MODULE and TYPE arrow pairs separately; rear power toggle and pedal push-down switch are additional. Follow-up2026-10-17: named type-table sum74 excludes bypass and TOTAL (not an effect module). Drive comprises30distortion/amp voices plus3acoustic variants; introductory “33distortion” is broader terminology. Sampler and2secondHoldDelay are separate functions. SmartMedia requires3.3V16–128MB and in-unit formatting; a computer needs a separate reader. Exact-card maximum aggregate sampling, persistent loop behavior, synth evidence and current software support remain incomplete. No bass/vocal membership inferred. No dependencies, software or manual files downloaded locally.

Earlier mixed HopeMusic707ii/708ii/GFX-3 shop ask is not assigned to this exact model. The reused Zoom707II URL previously indexed as POD HD500X remains rejected as identity-ambiguous; it is not a second corroborating707II offer.

### 708II — live controls and expression ranges

| Field | Own manufacturer manual evidence / qualification |
|---|---|
| Live / bypass, mute and tuning | Briefly pressing/releasing both footswitches gives **dry bypass with pedal volume control**. Holding both **at least one second**, then releasing, mutes OUTPUT/PHONES. Tuner reference **435–445 Hz in 1 Hz steps**, resetting to **440 Hz on power-up**. [Own manual p11][708-M] |
| Expression / per-patch range and toe exception | **PDLMIN/PDLMAX each 0–10**, stored per patch; reversed endpoints reverse the sweep. WAH/PITCH assignments temporarily replace the current MODULATION type with P-WAH/P-PIT. Storing the assignment saves that module ON. Toe press toggles the assigned module/sampler; **VOL has no toe-switch action**. [Own manual pp23–25][708-M] |

### 708II — sampler persistence and routing

| Field | Own manufacturer manual evidence / qualification |
|---|---|
| Looper / sample storage versus loop points | **Card recordings save automatically; internal recordings cannot be saved.** A–B start/end points reset when changing sample number or toggling sampler mode. Recorded audio persistence therefore does not establish persistent loop boundaries. [Own manual p15][708-M] |
| Looper / input and simultaneous restrictions | **BASS samples the effected input; AUX bypasses effects.** Sampler operation disables MOD and REV and cannot coexist with rhythm playback. Playback offers normal speed, half/quarter speed with corresponding pitch change, or half/quarter speed retaining pitch; these are playback modes, not additional algorithms. [Own manual pp14–15][708-M] |

### 708II — card backup and restore scope

| Field | Own manufacturer manual evidence / qualification |
|---|---|
| Memory / card SAVE exception | **SAVE requires an internally stored patch**; unsaved edits cannot be exported. An occupied target patch is overwritten. **If the target card group is empty, SAVE stores the entire USER group**, not merely the selected patch. Five groups of60 patches are external storage, not extra onboard recall locations. [Own manual pp28–29][708-M] |
| Memory / bulk restore and card utilities | LOAD overwrites one USER destination; **ALLOAD overwrites the entire USER group**. ALSAVE replaces the selected card group. P DEL removes a card group; SP DEL removes a sample; FORMAT erases all card data. These are documented scopes; no operation was performed. [Own manual pp28–32][708-M] |

### 708II — patch-linked rhythm and sample qualifications

| Field | Own manufacturer manual evidence / qualification |
|---|---|
| Drums / patch synchronization | **PATCH SYNC must be ON** for stored rhythm assignments to follow patches. An already-playing rhythm completes its current measure before switching. Intro patterns start on patch selection. **Ending-pattern conflict:** p26 requires R/P to start; p27 says selection plays the ending and stops. Automatic ending triggering remains unresolved. [Own manual pp26–27][708-M] |
| Looper / patch sample assignment | Patches store card-sample number, playback method and level0–30. **Automatic-play wording is qualified:** pp26–27 introduce playback on selection, but p28 explicitly enables sampler mode and requires a footswitch press to play. Do not promise unattended sample triggering on patch recall. [Own manual pp26–28][708-M] |

### B2.1u — synth tracking and dry-path routing

Checked2026-10-18 against [original manual pp25,29–30][B21-M]. Manufacturer text only; no hardware test or downloads.

| Field | Evidence / qualification |
|---|---|
| Synth / tracking and variations | **MONO SYN is explicitly monophonic pitch tracking**. Its15 variations group five each of sawtooth,square andPWM; these are parameters within one of the three synth types,not15 additional algorithms. STDSYN hasfour variations andtrigger sensitivity; SYNTLK hasfour vowel pairs anddecay. Polyphonic capability of these lattertwo is unestablished. |
| Routing / synth dry-path exception | With STDSYN,SYNTLK orMONO SYN selected, **COMP/LIMIT andWAH/EFX inbefore-DRIVE position affect only theoriginal path,not thesynth sound**. ZNR/MIX controls original-versus-drive/synth balance andpost-module level. Do not describe allnine modules as processing every synth path identically. |
| Expression / response curves | Four RTM curves: **UP** maps raised→depressed to minimum→maximum; **DOWN** reverses it; **HIGH** maps the saved patch value→maximum; **LOW** maps minimum→the saved patch value. These apply to the marked WAH/EFX, ZNR/MIX, MOD/SFX, DELAY and REVERB targets; the target parameter depends on effect type. Bypass makes the built-in pedal control volume regardless of assignment. [Own manual pp20,34][B21-M] |
| Expression / calibration and toe threshold | Power on holding PEDAL ASSIGN: **dn**. Fully raise pedal, press STORE: **UP**. Press fully down, release, then press STORE again; **Er** means repeat from the raised-pedal step. The manual explicitly says the module on/off switching point stays fixed regardless of the full-down adjustment action; calibration is not a documented adjustable toe-switch threshold. [Own manual pp20–21][B21-M] |
| Expression / blend and ZNR bypass | Built-in pedal’sDRIVE MIX assignment controlsZNR/MIX blend; it hasno effect whenDRIVE/SYNTH isoff. **ZNR/MIX cannot be disabled withthefootswitch**; noise reduction alone isdisabled bysettingZNR toOFF. This doesnot remove theblend/levelstage or establish asecondexternalpedalsocket. |

|---|---|
| Processing / cabinet parameter scope | For amp/drive types,CABINET **Off** retains head/stomp characteristics;**1–3** adds cabinet sound atdifferent intensities. For thethree synth types,the manual instead describes1–3 as different cabinet types. These parameter values are not three additional independent effect blocks,userIR slots oran audited complete cabinet-model list. |

The parameter-table reading example,p26,prints1.0–5.0 initsrange but1.0–2.0 initsstep explanation;that example conflict does not override the dedicated table. Five-second delay is not five-second overdub-loop storage orproof ofpatch-change spillover. |

### 607 — converters, analog levels and patch-write limits

Checked 2026-10-18 from the [original manufacturer manual](https://zoomcorp.com/media/documents/E_607.pdf), code607-5000-1; extracted text only,no local download or visual inspection.

| Field | Evidence / qualification |
|---|---|
| Processing / converters | P38: **20-bit ADC,64×oversampling;20-bit DAC,8×oversampling;31.25 kHz** sampling. These are converter specifications,not a USB audio format. |
| I/O / analog ratings | P38: mono input **−20 dBm rated,470 kΩ**; shared stereo line/headphone output **+5 dBm maximum into≥10 kΩ**. Source defines0 dBm=.775 Vrms; retain its published unit rather than silently relabel dBu. Rated input is not maximum input. |
| Power / battery switching | Pp5–8: mono INPUT plug switches battery operation on; unplugging prevents drain. Connecting the specified adapter powers the unit. Batteries cannot be recharged in607. Power-up enters Play irrespective of selector position. |
| Memory / edited patch persistence | Pp17–19: unsaved edits revert when another patch is selected. STORE chooses a target,then STORE confirms; destination is overwritten. User-created contents cannot be recovered by factory recall. VALUE± or moving PLAY/EDIT cancels storage. |

Current source supports the existing bass classification; mixed606/607 seller variations still do not establish an exact607 price.



### 607 — staged patch recall and fixed routing

Checked 2026-10-18 against original text in the [manufacturer manual, printed pp20–24](https://zoomcorp.com/media/documents/E_607.pdf),607-5000-1. No download or device operation.

| Field | Evidence / qualification |
|---|---|
| Live / patch preselection | Default selection changes sound immediately. Powering on while holding the up patch switch enables preselect: navigate to the flashing target without changing sound,then press both patch switches together to confirm. VALUE± changes bank only during selection. Power cycling restores direct selection;this is not a persistent scene mode or proven seamless spillover. |
| Routing / module order | Eight modules form a fixed serial chain:COMP/LIMIT→WAH→DRIVE→EQ→ZNR→AMP→MOD/PITCH→DLY/REV. Modules can be bypassed;the manual does not describe freely reordering them. Composite Octaver→Chorus helps explain why simultaneous effect count differs from module count. |
| Memory / selective factory recall | All Initialize restores all42 patches;Factory Recall restores a selected location and can be repeated. Both overwrite affected user edits;neither recovers previously overwritten user-created sounds. This is restoration of factory content,not a separate protected preset bank. |


### 607 — category-count reconciliation

Checked 2026-10-18 against the [manufacturer manual, printed pp24–38](https://zoomcorp.com/media/documents/E_607.pdf),607-5000-1;original text only,no download or device operation.

| Field | Evidence / qualification |
|---|---|
| Category choices / derived35-count basis | **2 dynamics+2 wah+11 drive+3 amp simulations+13 MOD/PITCH+4 delay/reverb=35**. This matches the headline if EQ and ZNR utility processing are excluded. It is a derived accounting reconciliation,not an explicit manufacturer definition or proof of35unique DSP algorithms. |
| Processing / internal variants | EQ’s50 patterns andContour are settings of its EQ module. Amp-with/without-ZNR selections are combinations of the three amp characters andnoise reduction. Bass Synth’snine character/blend patterns andPitch Shift’snine patterns are internal variations. Off andthese combinations are not silently counted as additional independent algorithms. |
| Processing / concurrency distinction | Octaver→Chorus is one selectable composite entry containing two serial effects. Thus menu choices,module positions andsimultaneous effects are different counts;the advertised nine does not mean nine freely selectable/reorderable modules. |

### 607 — tuner and master-volume persistence, 2026-10-18

[Own607 manual](https://zoomcorp.com/media/documents/E_607.pdf),607-5000-1,printed pp9,12–13. Text evidence;no device test.

| Field | Verified behavior / qualification |
|---|---|
| Live / tuner calibration | Reference **435–445Hz**,display35–45;power cycling restores **440Hz**. This is calibration range,not a measured bass-note detection range. |
| Live / tuner and bypass access | Tuner requires **PLAY** selector position. Press/release both patch switches for bypass,hold both at least1second for mute;either switch returns to Play. These mode labels do not establish analog/relay true bypass or effect-tail retention. |
| I/O / master volume | Hold both VALUE keys over1second to access **0–50** master volume;power cycling resets it to **40**. Manual explicitly permits headphone listening adjustment. This is global output adjustment,not extra patch memory or stored preset gain. |

<!-- comparison evidence ends -->

Maximum line output+3dBm into≥10kΩ;phones20+20mW into32Ω. Manufacturer dBm notation retained,not silently relabeled dBu. Response20Hz–40kHz,+1/−4dB into10kΩ. Not USB audio specifications. [Manual p27][B1XO-M] |
| Live / rhythm controls | Rhythm tempo40–250BPM,level0–30. Rhythm mode prevents patch switching/master adjustment;play mode permits rhythm but not pattern/level/tempo adjustment. Bypass/mute can retain rhythm. External footswitch tempo control is B1-only. [Manual pp10–11][B1XO-M] |
| Power / battery / USB | **9VDC300mA center-negative AD-0006 supply**,or4AA alkaline,approx12h;nonrecharging. Actual draw,barrel size/USB operation unverified. [Manual pp2,27][B1XO-M] |
| Dimensions / mass | **234W×155D×52Hmm;600g without batteries**. Own B1X row,not narrower B1. [Manual p27][B1XO-M] |
| IR / NAM / capture | User IR,native/converted NAM and proprietary capture unverified. |
| Editor / platforms | Retrieved discontinued B1X page lists manuals;current applications directory lists Guitar Lab for FOUR,not proof of original B1X compatibility. Own editor/updater OS remains unverified. No USB appears in the complete manual connector inventory;do not infer USB/audio support from sibling products. [Own product][B1XO-P]; [Applications][B1XO-A]; [Manual pp4–5][B1XO-M] |
| Live / patch preselect | Optional preselect chooses the next patch without changing sound;press both main switches to commit selection. Power cycle restores immediate switching. This is patch recall,not scene architecture. [Manual p16][B1XO-M] |
| Preset / unsaved edits | Changing patches discards unsaved edits. Factory locations are read-only;STORE from a factory patch defaults to user A0. [Manual pp13–15][B1XO-M] |
| Live / practice | **40 rhythm patterns**;REVERB/DELAY unavailable during rhythm playback. Looper/scenes unverified. [Manual pp25,27][B1XO-M] |
| Expression / calibration | Hold PEDAL ASSIGN while powering on: display **dn**; fully raise treadle and press STORE: **UP**; press fully down, release, then press STORE again. **Er** means repeat from the raised-pedal step. The displayed labels do not reverse the prescribed physical order. [Own manual p26][B1XO-M] |
| Live / master and tuner persistence | Master level display spans **0–98, then1.0** and resets to **80 at power-on**; this is distinct from saved patch level. Tuner reference **435–445 Hz** resets to **440 Hz at power-on**. Briefly pressing both main switches selects bypass; holding at least1s selects mute, unavailable in edit mode. [Own manual pp6–9][B1XO-M] |

### RFX-1000 — banks and combination limits

Checked2026-10-17 against [own manual][R1K-M],pp7–15.

| Bank | Eleven selections |
|---|---|
| Reverb | Hall;Room;Plate;Vocal;Ambience;Dimension;Percussion;Ensemble;Power;Gate;Reverse |
| Effects | Comp Lim;Delay;Pitch;Chorus;Flanger;Phaser;TRM-PAN;DLY+REV;CHO+REV;DLY/REV;FLG/REV |
| Mix&SFX | Power Mix;Wide Mix;Boost Mix;Vocal Mix;Mic Simulator;Cabinet Sim;Rotary;Ring Mod;Resonance;Lo-Fi EFX;Vocoder |

“+” means serial;“/” places first effect left,reverb right. Delay alone≤1486ms;both delay/reverb combinations≤743ms,fixed feedback. CHO+REV fixes rate;FLG/REV fixes depth.

Reverb selections1–9 offer11 character choices;Gate/Reverse instead adjust threshold/sensitivity. Preserve advertised363 selector combinations without treating everyposition as an independent named patch. Mix&SFX generally recommends fully wet output. Cabinet Sim models combo/stack tone;user IR import remains unverified.

### MS-80IR+ — IR slots and USB revision

Checked 2026-10-17 from the [effect list][M80-FX], ©2024 Z2I-5353-01, and [IR Loader manual][M80-IR], version1.02, ©2024 Z2I-5502-01. No downloads or visual inspection.

| Field | Evidence / gaps |
|---|---|
| Processing / IR routing | One user IR **or** one studio ambience per patch; selecting the IR bypasses the amp cabinet. Thus the documented three-slot arrangement is amp + (ambience or IR) + other effect, not four independent slots. |
| Category choices | TOOL: Noise Gate, ZOOM Noise Reduction, Stereo Guitar Graphic EQ (6-band), Stereo Guitar Graphic EQ7, Stereo Parametric EQ, Stereo Low EQ, Stereo High EQ, BPM. DELAY: Delay, Analog Delay, Tape Echo, Stereo Delay. These reconcile the twelve other entries; **BPM is tempo control, not another audio algorithm**. Amp and ambience names are enumerated below. |
| Delay / live | Delay and Analog Delay reach4s; Tape Echo2s; Stereo Delay2s independently per side. All four have selectable tails; Analog Delay offers mono/ping-pong. |
| IR loading / software | Windows/Mac loader adds, deletes and renames IRs. WAV44.1–192kHz accepted; bit depth, maximum length and stereo handling unestablished. App-side IR pool stores copies; dragging to hardware slots transfers them. Names allow20characters. Data-capable USB cable required. This is IR management, not evidence of a complete desktop patch editor. |
| USB / firmware | [Version1.10 supplement][M80-110], ©2025 Z2I-5744-01: USB MODE selects MIDI+AUDIO or MIDI-only. Some USB-MIDI controllers/converters require the latter, which disables USB audio. The supplement describes this USB change; it does not establish introduction of IR loading in1.10. |

### MS-80IR+ — live output and USB monitoring

Checked2026-10-17 against the [own operation manual][M80-M], Z2I-5301-02, sections R OUT POSITION, PRESELECT and audio interface.

| Field | Evidence / gaps |
|---|---|
| I/O routing | R OUT POSITION can tap within the chain, including **between amp head and cabinet**. Manufacturer example sends cabinet/effect-processed L to PA and amp-head-only R to guitar amp. This is a selectable tap, not an independent second amp chain. |
| Presets / live | PRESELECT keeps the current patch sounding while another is browsed; footswitch confirmation recalls it. This is patch preselection, not evidence of snapshots or gapless recall. |
| USB audio / recording | USB sends two post-effects channels; host playback enters after processing. USB REC GAIN spans−15 to+15dB. USB MONITOR blends direct/host0–100 and resets to50 at startup. Windows requires the own driver; Mac/phones/tablets need none per manual. No dry USB reamp channel established. |
| USB / MIDI control | USB MIDI CH selects reception channel; OMNI receives all channels. Exact program-number mapping and CC/expression implementation remain unverified. |

### G1Xnext — saving and live recall

Checked2026-10-17 against [original manual text][G1XN-M], pp9–10,15–18, G1N/G1XN-5000-1.

| Field | Evidence / gaps |
|---|---|
| Factory / user memory | All Initialize restores original user-patch contents, overwriting saved edits. This establishes factory originals, but not their exact names or equality to read-only presets. |
| Preset saving | Edits are lost when another patch is recalled unless stored. STORE selects a destination; a second STORE commits. Only user banksA–d accept writes; starting from preset banks0–3 defaults the destination toA0. Turning VALUE cancels before confirmation. |
| Presets / live recall | Preselect is enabled at startup with the up footswitch held. Browsing leaves current sound active; pressing both footswitches confirms recall. Power cycling restores ordinary selection. This does not prove gapless switching or snapshots. |
| Tuner / bypass | In play/rhythm modes, both switches briefly activate bypass; holding reaches mute. Unavailable in edit mode. Uneven switch timing can momentarily recall an adjacent patch. |
| Power / retained settings | Master level resets to80 and tuner reference to440Hz on restart; patch storage does not imply persistence of these global settings. |

| Processing / categories | MOD20:chorus,vintage chorus,flanger,phase,tremolo,auto pan,auto wah,pedal wah,step,pitch,pedal pitch,ring modulator,slow attack,filter,delay,echo,time trip,tremolo+chorus,ring+vibrato,vibe. REV:Hall,Room,Ping-Pong Delay,Delay+Hall,Delay+Room,andJAM. EQ has two editing pages,not two alternative algorithms. TOTAL/PEDAL are settings,not additional audio-effect modules. |
| Looper / sampler | Sampler shares guitar/AUX input;BP records without effects,EF with available effects. Pedal controls recording level;half-speed playback preserves pitch. Sampler excludes rhythm/JamPlay and disablesMOD/REV;leaving sampler erases its recording. JamPlay is a REV choice,not an extra independent block. |
| Preset / live | DirectLoadOFF browses silently until both footswitches confirm;setting saves automatically. BankHold confines footswitch browsing to current bank,but resets at power-cycle. Rhythm settings also reset;EasyEdit unavailable during rhythm playback. |

### Original G1 pre-select, rhythm and tuner limits

Checked 2026-10-18 using the original G1/G1X manual; only G1-applicable behavior recorded. No files downloaded or hardware tested.

| Field | Finding / qualification | Evidence |
|---|---|---|
| Controls / pre-select | Hold the up footswitch during power-up to enable pre-select. Browsing patches then leaves the current sound active; press both footswitches to confirm the flashing selection. Restart restores normal immediate switching. G1 can also browse banks with FS01 before confirmation. | [Own manual p16][G1O-M] |
| Looper / rhythm restrictions | Rhythm mode prevents patch changes and master-level adjustment. In play mode rhythm can run, but pattern, rhythm volume and tempo cannot be adjusted there. Rhythm tempo spans 40–250 bpm; volume 0–30. Rhythm can continue in bypass/mute; reverb remains unavailable while it plays. These rhythm functions do not establish a phrase looper. | [Own manual pp10–11][G1O-M] |
| Tuner / reference reset | Adjustable A reference 435–445 Hz; power-cycling resets it to 440 Hz. Tuning requires bypass or mute. | [Own manual pp8–9][G1O-M] |

### G2.1Nu — controls, USB and loop restrictions

Checked2026-10-18 against [manufacturer-authored manual copy][ZNU-C], pp6–7,13–15,29; cover/model and section structure match the manufacturer-hosted manual. Generated mirror summaries/FAQ excluded. Text inventory,not visual inspection.

| Field | Evidence / qualification |
|---|---|
| K/F/B | **4/2/8**: module selector+three parameter knobs;two patch footswitches;two EFFECT TYPE/BANK keys,STORE/SWAP,DIRECT,RHYTHM,TAP,PEDAL ASSIGN,USB/GLOBAL. Power/eco selectors andtoe pressure additional. |
| I/O / complete panel inventory | INPUT,OUTPUT,CONTROL IN,USB,DC9V. CONTROL IN accepts FP01/FP02 orFS01;one shared socket,not simultaneous separate expression/footswitch inputs. USB connector subtype unverified. |
| USB / audio format | **16-bit;32/44.1/48kHz** recording/playback. Internal96kHz/32-bit processing does not establish that USB format. |
| Looper / recording limits | Approximately **5seconds**,with overdubbing. Activating looper disables **DELAY**. Recording can stop manually orafter tempo-derived note duration;rhythm can play concurrently. |
| Looper / erasure | Turning recording-time or tempo knob after recording **erases the phrase**. MIX adjusts phrase volume0–100. Persistence across power/preset changes unverified. |
| Power / eco behavior | With eco enabled,≈25minutes unused enters standby;guitar input prevents entry but is ignored once asleep. Any control operation wakes it. |


### G2.1Nu — category census and acoustic simulation

Checked2026-10-18 against [manufacturer-authored manual][ZNU-C], pp34–48,49,51. Counts are a manual-derived census,not an independently confirmed installed-firmware library.

| Field | Evidence / qualification |
|---|---|
| Categories / choices | **COMP3;EFX9;ZNR3;DRIVE30;EQ1;MODULATION21;DELAY5;REVERB7**. EQ Lo/Hi edit one six-band module. DRIVE comprises29 shared-parameter amp/distortion entries plusAco.Sim;not30 amps. REVERB includes EarlyRef andMultiTapD. |
| Processing / count discrepancy | Category entries total **79**,versus published **83 types**. TOTAL provides patch level,RTM andname settings;looper is separate. Whether these explain the difference is unverified;do not silently change the manufacturer total orclaim a reconciled83-entry census. |
| Synth / acoustic | **Aco.Sim** explicitly makes electric guitar sound acoustic,with Top/Body controls. Cry is talking-modulator-like;RingMod andpitch effects do not establish a dedicated oscillator synth. Synth remains unverified. |
| Drums / rhythm choices | **40 numbered rhythm entries**,including metronomes;not40 drum kits. **Rhythm mode disables REVERB**,independently ofthe looper disablingDELAY. |


### G2.1Nu — factory fill, cabinet settings and monitoring

Checked2026-10-18 against [own manufacturer manual][ZNU-C], pp22–26,29–30. Documentation only; no reset,recording ordevice operation.

| Field | Evidence / qualification |
|---|---|
| Factory / user memory | User banks **A–J ship with copies of the preset-bank sounds**. Thus200 locations do not establish200 distinct factory sounds. ALL INITIALIZE overwrites user banks. |
| Category choices / cabinets | Direct mode offers **MATCHED,COMBO,TWEED,STACK**. MATCHED follows DRIVE type;others represent2×12combo,4×10tweed,4×12stack. These are cabinet settings,not userIR slots. |
| Processing / total reconciliation inference | **79 category entries+4 Direct cabinet settings=83** arithmetically. This is a plausible counting explanation,not an explicit manufacturer statement;the counting convention remains unresolved. |
| USB / monitoring | Records effected guitar;output mixes guitar with DAW playback. Direct monitoring can be disabled;avoid simultaneous DAW echo-back withdirect monitoring. USB recording level is separately adjustable andretained acrosspower-off. No dry reamp channel established. |
| Live / preselect | PRE SELECT stages a patch until both footswitches confirm;editing/storing beforeconfirmation affects the current patch. Power cycle exits thismode. |
| External control / FS01 | Seven global assignments: **BP/MT,BANK UP,RHYTHM,BPM TAP,DL TAP,DL HOLD,DL MUTE**. Delay operations require DELAY enabled;assignment survivespower-off. |

|---|---|
| I/O / analog input andoutput ratings | Own manualp50: **input1MΩ,rated−20dBm;maximumlineoutput+5dBm into≥10kΩ;headphones20mW+20mW into32Ω**. Manualuses0dBm=0.775Vrms convention. Ratings arenot measured clipping/headroom results. [Specifications][ZNU-M] |
| Conversion / response andnoise | **20Hz–40kHz,+1/−3dB at10kΩload;120dB equivalent-input-noise S/N;−100dBm residualnoise**. Keep distinct noise metrics;not120dB measured complete-system dynamicrange. [Specifications][ZNU-M] |

| Field | Manufacturer evidence / qualification |
|---|---|
| Looper / stop versus clear | Own manualp15: DOWN stops playback;UP restarts. HoldingDOWN untilCLEAR deletesphrase. UP alternates overdub/playback;bothfootswitches return toplaymode. Stop isnot documented aserase;power/preset persistence stillunknown. [Own manual][ZNU-M] |
| Editor / sleep andimport boundaries | [Edit&Share manufacturer manual,revisionE5](https://zoomcorp.com/media/documents/EditShare_Operation_Manual_English_2.pdf),pp4,7,10,12: computersleep unsupported;restartapp aftersleep. Effectreordering appears onlyoncapabledevices. Cross-modelimport exceptions explicitly name G3/G3X/G5,notG2.1Nu;genericimport/export doesnot establish siblingpatch compatibility. G2.1Nu supportpage lists Edit&Share,butcurrentOS/CPU compatibility remainsunverified. |

### NEURIG — update-dependent loading and USB operation

Checked2026-10-18 against original manual Z2I-5930-01. Pre-release documentation; no installation ordevice operation.

| Field | Evidence / qualification |
|---|---|
| Editor / NAM and IR availability | The manual's app section describes SessionZ Pedal control and NAM/IR loading as **future-update functionality**. Product-page capability advertising therefore does not establish launch-day import availability. Exact update/version and supported NAM architectures beyond advertised A2 Lite remain unresolved. [Manual][NEU-M] |
| USB / audio driver and power selection | Windows instructions require **ZOOM NEURIG Driver**;Mac/mobile need no driver permanual. Minimum OS/package availability unverified. Audio+MIDI mode has USB/Battery power selection;Battery is specified for mobile hosts,USB for computers with adequate power. Selection is **not saved** andresets toUSB atstartup. Audio-driver support isnot editor support. [Manual][NEU-M] |
| Looper / time and stop modes | **Free** records until manual stop or45s maximum;timed recording uses1–64quarter notes. Stop modes:Quick immediate,End atloopend,Fade stops after15s. Power-off persistence,stereo limits andRIG-change behavior remain unverified. [Manual][NEU-M] |

### NEURIG — processing ceiling and parameter slots

Checked2026-10-18 against original manual Z2I-5930-01, sections on RIG effect count and parameter editing. Pre-release scope retained.

| Field | Evidence / qualification |
|---|---|
| Processing / selection ceiling | Effects exceeding the processing budget are **greyed out and unselectable**;tapping shows **Process Overflow** and individual effect loads. Change/delete effects to stay within **100%**. DNA drive/amp models consume substantial processing. Fourteen slots therefore do not guarantee every combination, and this is a selection restriction rather than documented overload bypass behavior. [Manual][NEU-M] |
| Memory / effect parameter slots | **Program mode stores up to three parameter settings per effect**;Stomp mode stores one. Stored parameter slots can be linked to program recall. These are variations within the existing RIG/effect architecture, not three extra effects or additional independent RIG memories. [Manual][NEU-M] |

### MS-100BT — save and reset behavior
